1b1(phal chen): @##//sangs rgyas phal pa cha zhes bya ba shan tu rgyas pa chen po'i mdo//bam po dang po//de nas tshe dpon gyi bu nor 
1b2(phal chen): bzangs mtshan mo'i lha mo rab tu dga' ba'i mig 'gro bar rnam par snang ba'i byang chub sems dpa'i rnam par thar pa kun tu bzang 
1b3(phal chen): po'i dga' ba shin tu yangs shing dri ma med pa'i shugs kyi rgyal mtshan de la mos par bya ba dang /khong du chud par bya ba dang 
1b4(phal chen): rtogs par bya ba dang /shes par bya ba dang /yid ches par bya ba dang /rab tu gzhol bar bya ba dang /rgyas par dga' ba dang 
1b5(phal chen): rjes su shes par bya ba dang /rjes su dran par bya ba dang /rjes sugazhol bar bya ba dang /bsgom pa byed cing /dge ba'i 
1b6(phal chen): bshes gnyen gyi gdams ngag la nan tan du bya ba dang /gdams ngag rjes su bstan pa la bar chad med par bya ba'i phyir mtshan 
1b7(phal chen): mo'i lha mo rab tu dga' pa'i mi 'gro bar rnam par snang pas/'gro bar gtams pa rjes su dran par byed cing /dge ba'i bshes gnyen blta 
1b8(phal chen): ba'i rjes su sems pa'i dbang po'i dkyil 'khor dang /dge ba'i bshes gnyenamathong ba thob par bya ba la yang dag par spyod pa/phyogs kun tu 
2a1(phal chen): @#//
2b1(phal chen): dkyil 'khor yod pa de la me'i rdul shin tu phra ba 'am/chu'i rdul shin tu phra ba 'am/sa'i rdul shin tu phra ba'am/rdo rje'i rdul shin tu phra ba 'am /nor bu rin po che'i rgyal po dam pa sna tshogs kyi rdul shin tu phra ba dang /
2b2(phal chen): me tog dang /spos dang /phye ma'i rdul shin tu phra ba 'am /rgyan thams cad kyi rdul shin tu phra ba'am /dmigs pa thams cad kyi rdul shinatu phra ba ji snyed cig yod pa'i rdul shin tu phra ba de dag thams cad la rdul phra mo re re 
2b3(phal chen): zhing yang 'jig rten gyi khams sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi rdul shin tu phra ba snyed/chu'i phung po dang bcas/me'i phung po dang bcas/rlung gi phung po dang bcas/sa'i phung po dang bcas/'jig rten gyi khams kyi mtshams
2b4(phal chen): dang bcas/bzhi dang bcas/rten dang bcas/dbyibs sna tshogs can/sa'i gzhi rnam pa tha dad pa sna tshogs dang ldan pa'i ri'i 'khor sna tshogs can/chu klung dang mtsho sna tshogs kyis brgyan pa/rgya mtsho sna 
2b5(phal chen): tshogs kyis kun nas 'khor ba/lha rdzas kyi khyim sna tshogs kyi rgyan dang ldan pa shing sna tshogs kyis yongs su gang ba/nam mkha'i dbyings kyi rgyan sna tshogs can/lha'i grong khyer gyi khyim gyi rgyan dang bcas pa/klu'i grong 
2b6(phal chen): khyer gyi khyim gyi rgyan dang ( )bcas pa/gnod sbyin gyi grong khyer gyi khyim gyi rgyanadang bcas pa/dri za'i grong khyer gyi khyim gyi rgyun dang bcas pa/lha ma yin gyi grong khyer gyi khyim gyi rgyandang bcas pa/nam mkha' lding gi 
2b7(phal chen): grong khyer gyi khyim gyi rgyan»dang bcas pa/mi 'am ci'i grong khyer gyi khyim gyi ( )rgyan dang bcas pa/lto 'phye chen po'i grong khyer gyi khyim gyi rgyan dang bcas pa/mi'i grong khyer gyi khyim gyi rgan dang bcas pa/phyogs dang phyogs 
2b8(phal chen): mtshams thams cad na rnam par gnas pa'i sems can thams cad kyi grong khyer gyi khyim gyi gnas dang /sems can dmyal ba'i 'jig rten gyi yul dang /byol song gi skye gnas kyi 'jig rten gyi yul dang /gshin rje'i 
3a1(phal chen): @#//'jig rten gyi yul dang /mi dang mi ma yin gyi lam gyi rgyud kyi shi 'pho ba dang /skye ba'i yul dang bcas pa/skye bo sna tshogs kyis ma 'dres pa'i rgyud mtha' yas pa'i bye brag la yongs su gzhol pa rnams 'du 
3a2(phal chen): zhing 'jig par gyur pa mthong ngo //'jig rten gyi khams de dag kyang rnam pa sna tshogs su mthong ste/'jig rten gyi khams la la ni kun nas nyon mongs par mthong ngo //la la ni yongs su dag par mthong ngo //la la shin tu 
3a3(phal chen): kun nas nyon mongs par ma thong ngo //la la ni lam gyi rgyud yongs su dag par mthong nga //la la ni kun nas nyon mong pa yongs su dag par mthong //la la ni rnam par dag pa kun nas nyon mongs par 
3a4(phal chen): mthong ngo //la la ni gzhi mnyam par mthong ngo //la la la ni spyi'u tshugs su gnas par mthong ngo //la ni snel bzhir 'dug par mthong ngo //'jig rten gi khams de dag gi sems can lam gyi rgyud dang 
3a5(phal chen): sems can gyi skye gnas thams cad na mtshan mo'i lha mo sems can kun tu skyong ba'i gze brjid dpal sems can so so nas 'dul ba'i rkyen bzhin du 'jig rten thams cad la ma 'dres par yongs su bstan pa'i phyir/sems 
3a6(phal chen): can thams cad la mngon du gyur par gnas par mthong ngo /sems can tshe'i tshad rnam pa ji lta bu dang ldan pa dang /mos pa'i spyod yul rnam pa sna tshogs can dang /lus rnam pa sna tshogs can dang/lus rnam pa ji lta bu dang 
3a7(phal chen): ldan pa dang /rig rnam par phye ba dang /nges pa'i tshig gi 'du byed kyi tha snyad rnam pa ji lta bu dang ldang pa dang /sbyor ba'i rnam pa ji lta bu dang lda pa dang /dbang gi rnam pa ji lta bu dang ldas pa rnams yongs su smyin par bya ba dang 
3a8(phal chen): gdul pa'i don du sems can thams cad la ma 'dresapar mngon sum du gnas par mthong ngo //de yang 'di lta ste/sems can dmyal ba'i lam gyi rgyud du gtogs pa'i sems can rnams kyi sems can dmyal ba'i 
3b1(phal chen): sdug bsngal dang/'jigs pa rnam par bzlog pa dang/byol song gi skye gnas sna tshogs su skyes pa'i sems can rnams kyi gcig la gcig za ba'i 'jigs pa rnam par bzlogs pa dang/gshin rje'i 'jig rten gyi lam
3b2(phal chen): gyi rgyud du gtogs pa'i sems can rnams gyi bkres pa dang/skom pa la stsogs pa'i sdug bsngal gyi 'jigs pa rnam par bzlo pa dang/klu'i 'jig rten gyi lam gyi rgyud du gtogs pa'i sem can rnams kyi klu'i sdug bsngal dang/
3b3(phal chen): 'jigs pa thams cad rnam par bzlog pa dang/'dod pa'i khams su gtogs pa'i sems can thams cad kyi 'dod pa'i sdug bsngal dang/'jigs ba rnam par bzlog pa dang /mi'i 'jig rten gyi lam gyi rgyad du gtogs pa'i sems
3b4(phal chen): can rnams kyi mtshan mo'i mun pa mun nag gis chod pa'i mun pa'i 'jigs pa thams cad rnams par bzlog pa dang/mi snyan pa dang/bsngags pa ma yin pa dang/grags pa ma yin pa'i sgra dang/tshigs su bcad pa la mngon
3b5(phal chen): par zhen pa'i sems can rnams kyi grags pa'i ma yin pa dang/bsngags pa ma pa yin pa'i 'jigs pa rnam par bzlog pa dang/'khor gyi 'jigs par ltung ba'i sems can rnams kyi 'khor tu bag tsha ba'i 'jigs pa rnam par
3b6(phal chen): bzlog pa dang/'chi bas 'jigs shing sdang ba'i sems can rnams gyi 'chi ba'i 'jigs pa rnam par ba zlog pa dang/ngan song gi g.yang sas 'jigs shing sdang ba'i ses can rnams kyi ngan song gi g.yang sas 'dzigs pa rnam par zlog
3b7(phal chen): pa dang/'chi ba med pas 'jigs shing skrag pa'i sems can rnams kyis 'khor du 'tshe ba med pa'i bag tsha ba'i 'jigs pa rnal par bzlog pa dang/dge ba'i rtsa ba chud zos kyis 'jigs shing bag tsha ba'i sems can rnams kyid ge
3b8(phal chen): ba'i rtsa ba chud zos kyi 'jigs pa rnam par bzlo pa dang/byang chub kyi sems nyams kyis 'jigs shing bag tsho ba'i sems can rnams kyibyang chub kyi sems nyams gyis 'jigs pa rnam par bzlod pa dang/sdig pa'i grogs po
4a1(phal chen): @#//
4b1(phal chen): med pa yang ma yin pa'i sems can thams cad yongs su skyob pa'i smon lam gyi stobs mngon par bsgrubs pa dang /byang chub sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin gyi shugs drag po dang /rnam par gnon pa shin tu yangs pa'i stobs dang /byang 
4b2(phal chen): chub sems dpa'i mngon par shes pa chen po'i stobs dang mthu dang /byang chub sems pa' kun tu bzang po'i spyod pa dang /smon lam mngon par bsgrubs pa'i stobs kyis snying rje chen po'i chu la rgya mtsho'i shugs drag po shin tu 
4b3(phal chen): skyes pa'i phyir 'gro ba thams cad du thogs pa med pa'i byams pa chen pos rgyas par dgang ba dang /sems can thams cad kyi bde ba yongs su bsgrub pa dang /dga' ba'i shugs rnam par 'phel bar bya ba dang /sems 
4b4(phal chen): can thams cad yongs su bsdu ba'i ye shes rab tu bsgrub pa'i phyir/byang chub sems dpa'i rnam par thar pa rgya chen po'i rnam par 'phrul ba dang /khyu mchog gi mthu phyung ba dang ldan pas/zhing thams cad yongs su dag 
4b5(phal chen): par bya ba la mngon tu gyur par gnas par mthong ngo //chos thams cad kyi rgyus shes par bya ba la mngon tu gyurapa dang /sangs rgyas thams cad mchod cing rim gror bya ba la mngon du gyur pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa thams 
4b6(phal chen): cad kyi bstan pa yongs su gzung ba la mngon du gyur pa dag/dge ba'i rtsa ba thams cad bstsags pa la mngon du gyur pa dang/byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa thams cad rnam par 'phel bar byab la mngon du gyur pa dang
4b7(phal chen): sems can thams cad kyi sems la sgrib pa med par mngon du gyur par gnas pa dang /sems can thams cad kyi dbang po yongs su smin par bya ba la mngon du gyurapar gnas pa dang /semasacan thams cad kyi mos pa 
4b8(phal chen): rgya mtsho rnam par spyad pa la mngon du gyur pa da /sems can thams cadakyi sgrib pa'i chos rnam par bzlog pa la mngon du gyur pa da/sems can thams cad mi shes pa'i mun pa rnam par bstsal ba la mngon du gyur cing
5a1(phal chen): @#//thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes snang ba yongs su bskyed pa'i don du gnas pa mthong ngo //de nas tshong dpon gyi bu nor bzangs kyis mtshan mo'i lha mo sems can kun tu skyong ba'i gzi brjid dpal gyi byang chub sems dpa'i 
5a2(phal chen): rnam par thar pa'i khyu mchog gi mthu phyung ba dang /rnam par 'phrul pa 'jig rten thams cad la mngon par gnas shing /'gro ba rnam par 'dul ba ston pa bsam gyis mi khyab pa de lta bur mthong nas rab tu dga' ba skyes te/
5a3(phal chen): spro ba chen po'i shugs drag po rgya mtsho rab tu thob nas/mtshan mo'i lha mo sems cad kun tu skyong ba'i gzi brdzid dpal la lus thams cad kyis phab ste phyag byas nas dor gyen du bltas so//de nas mtshan mo'i lha 
5a4(phal chen): mo sems can kun tu skyod ba'i gzi brjid dpal gyis byang chub sems dpa'i gzugs dang /mtshan rnam par dag pa de mi snang bar byas te/mtshan mo'i lha mo'i gzugs su byas ste/rnam par 'phrul pa thams cad 'byin 
5a5(phal chen): cing 'dug par gnas so//de nas tshong dpon gyi bu nor bzangs kyis mtshan mo'i lha mo sems can kun tu skyong ba'i gzi brjid dpal kyi mdun du 'dug ste/thal mo sbyar nas de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//
5a6(phal chen): khyod kyi sku yangs mtshan rab rgyan gyis brgyan//shin tu bzang zhing mdzes pa bdag gis mthong //nam mkha' skar ma'i tshogs kyis brgyan pa bzhin//dpe byad bzang pos rnam par bris pa mthong //'od kyi 'khor can sku 
5a7(phal chen): bzang dam pa'i mchog/mtha' yas zhing gi rdul khams mang po snyed//kha dog sna tshogs rnam pa'i dpe tshul du/phyogs rnams kun gyi dbyings su khyab par gang //'od gzer dra ba mang po 'gro sems mnyam//
5a8(phal chen): khyod kyi ba spu'i sgo nas 'gyed ma mdzad//'od gzer sgo la pad mo yid 'ong gnas//khyod kyi sprul pa 'gro ba'i sdug bsngal sel//spos kyi na bun sprin du chags pa 'gyed//'gro ba'i gzugs su gnas te kun nas mdas 
5b1(phal chen): me tog sgo rnams kun nas dgyes mdzad pas//chos dbyings bzhugs pa'i rgyal ba kun tu khyab//khyod kyi phrag pa'i lhun yangs 'phags pa ni//rin chen 'or 'phro ri bo ri rab 'dra//dri med de ni 'gro ba kun tu gsal//
5b2(phal chen): gti mug mun pa rnam par bzlog pa mdzad//khyod kyi dri med zhal mdog bzang ba las//na bun sprin dang nyi ma yangs pa 'byung //byod kyi nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor 'dra 'od bzang //rnam par snang mdzad spyod yul yangs pa 
5b3(phal chen): 'byin//khyod kyi spyan nas zla skar 'od 'byung ba//rtag tu dri med sprin ltar mang ba ste//de dag phyogs bcur bgang zhing 'gro ba rnams//rab tu snang byed 'jig rten rab rib sel//khyod kyi mtshan las 'gro ba'i lus 
5b4(phal chen): mnyam pa'i//sprul pa rgya mtsho phyogs su khyab par 'phro//de dag chos dbyings yangs par rgyas 'gengs shing //sems can tshad med tshogs rnams yongs su 'dul//khyod kyi sku ni 'byam klas phyogs su snang //
5b5(phal chen): 'gro ba kun la mngon 'byung dga' ba skyed//rgyal po mye dang rkun po chus bskyed pa'i//'jigs pa thams cad rnam sel zhi bar mdzad//gang tshe khyod kyi spyan sngar bdag btang nas//yon tan rnam par sems 
5b6(phal chen): shing nyer phyin pa//de'i tshe 'od gzer dri med 'khor bzang ba//khyod kyi zhal gyi smin mtshams gseb nas 'thon//phyogs rnams rgya mtsho brgya mang snang mdzad nas//'jig rten snang ba rgya che yongs bskyed de 
5b7(phal chen): rnam 'phrul rnam pa mang po rnam bstan nas//de tshe bdag gi lus kyi gseb tu zhi//gang tshe 'od kyi 'khor gyis bdag reg pa//de tshe bde ba rmad byung yangs pa myong //gzungs dang ting 'dzin brgya mang yongs su non 
5b8(phal chen): phyogs bcu'i rgyal ba'i dpag yas mthong bar 'gyur//gom pa rnam bor gyen du bteg pa la 'ang //rdul phran grangs snyed tshul ni bdag gis rig/rdul phran shin tu phra ba gcig la yang //zhing gi rdul 
6a1(phal chen): @#//snyed zhing rnams mthong bar gyur//rdul la gnas kyang rnam pa tha gu ste//zhing mang brgya ni shin tu nyon yang mongs//de'i 'gro ba sdug bsngal nyams su myong //cho nger du zhing smre sngags yongs 
6a2(phal chen): su 'don//zhing mang la la kun nas nyon mongs dag/de na sdug bsngal mang zhing bde ba chung //rgyal ba thugs rje can yang der skyes ste//rgyal ba'i nyan thos rang rgyal de bzhin no//zhing tshul la la yongs dag 
6a3(phal chen): nyon mongs ste//byang chub sems dpa'i gnas bzangs mang pos brgyan//skyes pa bud med yongs brgyan blta na sdug/skyo na mang rgyal ba'i gdung yang rgyun mi 'chad//zhing rnams rgya mtsho yangs shing dri med pa//
6a4(phal chen): rdul la gnas shing mnyam pa'i gzhi dang ldan//de dag bskal pa brgya mang sngon gyi tshe//rnam par snang mdzad spyod mdzad yongs su mdzad//zhing rnams 'byam klas kun na rgyal ba dag/shing dbang dam pa'i drung 
6a5(phal chen): du gshegs pa stan//rnam par 'phrul zhing byang chub 'tshang yang rgya//chos kyi 'khor lo bskor cing 'dul ba ston//rnam par snang mdzad spyo yul yangs pa la//khyod kyang kun tu rjes su zhugs par mthong //
6a6(phal chen): mchod pa stong phrag tshad med khrag khrig gis//rgyal ba kun la mngon par mchod mdzad do//de nas tshod dpon gyi bu nor bzangs kyis tshigs su bcad pa de dag smras nas/mtshan mo'i lha mo sems can kun tu skyong ba'i 
6a7(phal chen): gzi brjid dpal la 'di skad ces smras so//lha mo byang chub sems dpa'i rnam par thar pa de/de lta bu'i zab pa ni ngo mtshar chen/rnam par thar pa de'i ming ni ci zhes bgyi/de khyod kyis brnyes nas yun ni ji srid 
6a8(phal chen): cig lag dang //byang chub sems dpas ji ltar nan tan du bgyis na rnam par thar pa de yongs su dag par 'gyur//smras pa/rigs kyi bu gnas 'di ni lha dang bcas pa'i 'jig rten dang /nyan thos dang /rang sangs rgyas 
6b1(phal chen): su bcas pas yid ches par dka' ba ste//de ci'i phyir zhe na/'di ni byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang po'i spyod pa dang /smon lam khong du chud pa rnams kyi spyod yul te/'di ni byang chub sems pa'i snying rje chen por 
6b2(phal chen): lhur len pa/'gro ba thams cad yongs su bskyab pa la zhugs pa/mi khom pa dang /ngan song dang /ngan 'gro'i lam thams cad rnam par bzlog pa la zhugs pa/sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad sangs rgyas kyi zhing yongs 
6b3(phal chen): su dag pa/bla na med par bya ba la zhugs pa/sangs rgyas gyi zhing thams cad na bzhugs pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs rgyun mi gcad pa la zhugs pa/sangs rgya kyi bstan pa yongs su gzung ba la zhugs pa/
6b4(phal chen): bskal pa thams cad du byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa la gnas pas/gnas par bya ba'i smon lam chen po rgya mtsho la zhugs pa/chos rgya mtsho thams cad la sgrib pa med pa'i ye shes snang ba rnam par spyad pa la zhugs pa 
6b5(phal chen): skad cig re re zhing dus gsum gyi tshul brgya mtsho thams cad du ye shes snang bas/rnam par gnas pa rab tu thob pa rnams kyi yul te/'on kyang de bzhin gshegs pa'i byin gyi rlabs kyi bstan par bya'o//rigs kyi 
6b6(phal chen): bu sngon 'das pa'i dus bskal pa sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi rdul shin tu phra ba snyed cig gi pha rol tu 'jig rten gyi khams rnam par snang mdzad gzi brjid dpal zhes bya bar bskal pa rdul dang bral ba'i dkyil 'khor ces bya ba phyung ste 
6b7(phal chen): bskal pa de dag la sangs rgyas ri rab kyi rdul shin tu phra ba snyed gcig byung bar gyur to//'jig rten gyi khams rnam bar snang mdzad gzi brjid gzi brjid dpal zhes ba ba de ni rin po che thams cad kyi sprin gyi rgyan gyis brgyan pa ste/
6b8(phal chen): rdo rje las byas pa'i gzhal med khang dang /gnas kyis yongs su mdzes par byas pa'o//'jig rten gyi khams de'i gzhi ni rin po che'i rgyal po dri ma med pa'i 'od rgya mtsho las blangs pa ste/lhun dang lus ni nor bu rin po che 
7a1(phal chen): @#//spos thams cad kyi rgyal po las gyur pa'o//de ni dbyibs kun tu zlum pa ste/rnam par dag cing kun nas nyon mongs pa/lhab lhub thams cad sprin gyi bla res kun nas khyab pa/rin po che'i 'khor yug 
7a2(phal chen): rgyan thams cad dang ldan pa/rim pa stong gis yongs su bskor ba/gling bzhi pa bye ba khrag khrig brgya phrag stong gi shin tu rgyas par rgyan pa ste/de la gling bzhi pa la la ni kun nas nyon mongs pa ste/sems can kun nas nyon 
7a3(phal chen): mongs pa'i las dang ldan pa rnams kyi gnas kyi gnas so//la la ni kun nas nyon mong shing rnam par dag pa ste/sems can las 'dren ma dang ldan pa rnams kyi gan sa so//la la ni rnam par dag cing kun na sa nyon mongs 
7a4(phal chen): pa ste/sems can yongs su dag cing kun nas nyon mogs pa dge ba'i rtsa ba sbyangs pa/kha na ma tho ba nyung ba rnams kyi gnas so//la la la ni shin tu yongs su dag pa ste/byang chub sems dpa' dag pa rnams kyi gnas so 
7a5(phal chen): 'jig rten gyi khams rnam par snang mdzad gzi brjid dpal zhes bya ba de'i shar phyogs kyi 'khor yug gi mtshams na gling bzhi pa rin po che'i me tog sgrol ma'i rgyal mtshan zhes bya ba rnam par dag cing kun nas nyon mongs pa 
7a6(phal chen): ma rmos ma bskyed pa'i 'bras bu la yongs su spyod pa/sngon gyi las kyi rnam par smin pas mngon par grub par khang pa brtsegs pa'i gnas dang /gzhal med khang la yongs su spyod pa/'khor kun nas yid bzhin gyi shing 
7a7(phal chen): gis 'khor ba/spos kyi shing sna tshogs kyi lhun las spos kyi sprin rgyun myi 'chad par 'byung ba/phreng ba sna tshogs kyi shing las phreng ba'i sprin rgyun mi 'chad par 'byung ba/me tog sna tshogs kyi shing las me tog kha dog 
7a8(phal chen): dang dre bsam gyis myi khyab pa'i sprin rgya tshor rgyun myi 'chad par 'bab pa phye ma dang kha dog sna tshogs kyi shing gi lhun las spos rin po che'i rgyal po thams cad kyi phye ma'i char rgyun mi 'chad par 'bab pa/rin po che'i shing sna 
7b1(phal chen): tshogs las rin po che'i 'bru mang po 'brul ba'i kha dog snang bas shin tu snang bar byas pa/lha rdzas kyi rol mo sna tshogs gi shing las sil snyan sna tshogs kyi sprin rlung gis bskyod cing nam mkha'i dbyings las sgra dbyangs 
7b2(phal chen): snyan pa 'byung pa/nyin mtshan gyi bde ba nyi ma dang zla bas dbyung ba/nor bu rin po che kun tu snang bas gzhi byas pa'i sa rab tu mnyam par gyur pa ste/gling bzhi po der rgyal po'i pho brang bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong byung bar 
7b3(phal chen): gyur te/rgyal po'i pho brang de dag re re zhing yang chu klung stong gis yongs su bskor ba'o//chu klung de dag thams cad kyang lha rdzas kyi me tog sil mas gang ba'o//lha rdzas kyi rol mo dang glu'i sgra skad snyan cing yid du 'ong 
7b4(phal chen): ba 'byin pa'o//chu 'gram rin po che'i shing yid du 'ong bas rnam par brgyan pa'o//rin po che sna tshogs kyis shin tu brgyan pa'i gru phan tshun rgyu ba'o//bde ba sna tshogs ci dga' bar yongs su spyad du yod pa'o//
7b5(phal chen): chu rlung gi mtshams kyi gzhi re re dag na'ang grong khyer bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong phrag bcu kun nas gnas pa ste/grong khyer de dag re re zhing yang grong rdal bye ba khrag khrig brgya phrag stong gis yongs su skor ba'o//grong dang 
7b6(phal chen): grong khyer dang grong rdal de dag thams cad kyang lta rdzas kyi bza' shing gi ra ba dang /gzhal med khang dang /khang pa bye ba khrag khrig brgya phragastong gi 'khor gyis yongs su bskor ba'o/gling bzhi pa'i de'i 'dzam bu'i gling 
7b7(phal chen): gi dbus na rgyal po'i pho brang dbu ma rin po che'i me tog sgron ma zhes bya ba yod par gyur to/de ni skyid cing longs spyod phun sum tshogs pa dang ldan la/rtag tu lo legs shing bde ba'i rnam pa thams cad dang ldan pa'o 
7b8(phal chen): lha dang myi mang pos yongs su gang ba'o//sems can dge ba bcu'i las kyi lam dang ldan pa rnams kyi gnas su gyur pa ste/rgyal po'i pho brang rin po che'i me tog sgronam zhes bya ba de na rgyal po rnam par snang ba rin 
8a1(phal chen): @#//chen pad mo dpal gyi gtsug phud snying po zhes bya ba 'khor los bsgyur ba/gling bzhi la dbang ba pad mo'i snying po las rdzus te byung ba/myi chen po'i mtshan sum bcu rtsa gnyis kyis lus shin tu brgyan pa 
8a2(phal chen): chos dang ldan pa/chos kyi rgyal po rin po che bdun dang ldan pa gnas par gyur to//de'i bu dpa' zhing rtul phod la yan lag gi dam pa'i gzugs dang ldan pa/gzhan gyi 'khor rab tu 'jom pa/rnam pa thams cad yongs su 
8a3(phal chen): tshang ba'i yan lag dam pa dang ldan pa stong tshang bar 'byung ngo //de'i btsun mo'i 'khor gyi bud med kyang bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong phrag bcu yod par gyur te/de dag kun kyang 'khor los sgyur ba'i skal pa dang mnyam pa'i dge 
8a4(phal chen): ba'i rtsa ba las byung ba 'thun par spyod pa rin po che'i rgyan dang 'khrungs su byung ba/dge ba'i sems dang ldan pa/lha'i bu mo dang khyad par med par 'dra ba'i gzugs can/'dzam bu chu klung gi gser gyi kha dog 
8a5(phal chen): dang ldan pa'i lus can/lus kyi ba spu las lha rdzas kyi spos sna tshogs 'byung ba/lus las lha rdzas kyi 'od dri zhim po dri ma med par rab tu 'gyed pa sha stag go/de'i blon po bye ba phrag bcu byung ste blon po rin po che 
8a6(phal chen): las stsogs pa dag go/'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal po rnam par snang ba rin chen pad mo dpal gyi gtsug phud snying po de'i btsun mo dam pa bud med rin po che dpal gyi bzhin yongs su rgyas pa zhes byas gzugs mdzes sha 
8a7(phal chen): sdug la/blta na yid du 'ong ba kha dog bzang po shin tu rgyas pa'i dam pa dang ldan pa/skra mthon ting du 'dug pa/mig dkar nag 'byes pa/mdog gser gyi kha dog du 'dug pa/tshangs pa'i dbyangs dang ldan pa/
8a8(phal chen): lus mas 'od rtag tu 'byung ba/'khor kun tu dpag chad stong gi tshad du 'od kha dog du ma dang ldan pa/lha rdzas kyi dri zhim po can 'gyed pa byung ngo //rgyal po'i brtsun mo dpal gyi bzhin yongs su rgyus pa zhes bya ba 
8b1(phal chen): de las bu mo pad mo bza' mo mig yid 'ong ba'i dpal gyi zla ba zhes bya ba yan lag thams cad chad pa/gzugs ma rjes shing sdug pa/blta na yid du 'ong ba 'jig rten thams cad kyis blta na mi dmas pa byung bar gyur to//rigs 
8b2(phal chen): kyi bu 'di lta ste dper na/'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal po la blta na su yang ngoms par mi 'gyur bde bzhin du 'khor los sgyur ba'i bu mo pad mo bzang mo'i mig yid du 'ong ba'i dpal de la blta bas kyang su yang ngoms par mi 'gyung 
8b3(phal chen): te/shes rab kyis yongs su ngoms par 'gyur ba ni ma gtogs so//de'i tshe de'i dus na/sems can rnams kyis tshe dpag tu med par gyur te/de la gcig tu nges pa med pas bar ma dor 'chi ba ni yod do//de'i 
8b4(phal chen): dus na sems can rnams kyi dbyibs sna tshogs dang /kha dog sna tshogs dang /dbyangs sna tshogs dang /ming sna tshogs dang /rigs sna tshogs dang /tshe'i tshad sna tshogs dang /chu zheng rnam pa tha dad pa dang /mos pa dang 
8b5(phal chen): stobs dang /brtson 'grus dang /mthu rnam pa tha dad pa dang /yid du 'ong ba dang /yid du mi 'ong ba rnam pa tha dad pa dang yangs pa dang dma' ba la mos pa sna tshogs kyang yod par gyur te/de la sems can ji snyed 
8b6(phal chen): cig mdog bzang ba dang /yangs pa la mos pa dang /yan lag tshang ba dang /blta na sdug cing yid du 'ong bar gyur pa de dag ni 'di skad ces mdzer te/kye mi dag de ni kha dog med pas ches bzang ngo zhes zer ro//
8b7(phal chen): de bzhin du sems can ji snyed cig lus kyi dbyibs legs pa dang ldan par gyur pa de dag kyang /sems can lus kyi dbyibs mi sdug par 'gyur ba rnams la rlom zhing khyad du gsod do//de dag de ltar gcig la gcig 
8b8(phal chen): khyad du gsod pa las byung ba'i mi dge ba'i rtsa bas tshe'i tshad las kyang yongs su bri bar gyur te/kha dog dang stobs dang bde ba las kyang yongs su bri ba gyur to//rgyal po'i pho brang rin po che'i me tog sgron ma 
9a1(phal chen): @#//de'i byang phyogs logs na/snying po byang chub kyi shing kun tu grags pa'i chos kyi sprin sgra'i rgyal mtshan zhes bya ba de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi snying po byang chub kyi rgyan skad cig re re la yongs su ston nas/
9a2(phal chen): rtsa ba rin po che'i rgyal po rdo rje ltar sra zhing myi 'jig pa/nor bu rin po che thams cad las grub pas lhun shin tu yangs shing rgyas pa/sdong po dang yal ga dang lo ma dang /mdab ma dang me tog dang /'bras bu rin po che thams
9a3(phal chen): cad kyi rgyu las grub pa/kun tu cha mnyam par bkod cing /kun tu khyab pa'i yan lag dang ldan pa/rgyan kun tu rgyas par 'gengs shing mi zad pa dang ldan pa/rin po che sna tshogs kyi 'od 'phro ba dang 'od gzer
9a4(phal chen): kun tu snang bar 'gyed pa/de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi yul rnam par 'phrul pa'i dbyangs rjes su sgrog pa yod de/snying po byang chub de'i mdun rol na/spos chu'i mtsho rin po che'i me tog dang /klog dang chos kyi
9a5(phal chen): 'brug sgra'i sprin gyi dbyangs shes bya ba yod de/de ni rin po che'i shing bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong phrag bcus kun nas bskor ba/rin po che'i shing de dag thams cad kyang byang chub kyi shing gi dbyibs su 'khod pa sha stag go/
9a6(phal chen): spos chu mtsho chen po rin po che'i me tog dang glog dang chos kyi 'brug sgra'i sprin gyi dbyang shes bya ba de'i 'gram thams cad kyang nor bu rin po che thams cad bkram zhing rnam par bkod pa ste/rin po che thams cad kyi phreng ba sna
9a7(phal chen): tshags su dgrigs pa/rin po che rnam par dag pa las byas pa'i khang pa sna tshogs kyi rgyan gyis yongs su brgyan pa'o//rgyan rnam par dag pa sna tshogs kyi rgyan gyis shin tu brgyan pa'o//snying po byang chub de'i 'khor thams
9a8(phal chen): cad kyang nor bu rin po cha'i khang pa brtsegs pa chen po bsam gyis mi khyab pa dang ldan pa/pad ma'i snying po can sha stag gis yongs su 'khor ba/spos chu'i mtsho chen po rin po che'ime tog dang /glog dang chos kyi 'brug sgri'i
9b1(phal chen): sprin gyi dbyangs shes bya ba de'i dbus nas/pad mo'i rgyal po lo ma'i mtshams nas dus gsum gyi de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi yul rnam par ston pa'i 'od gzer gyi sprin gyi rgyan ces bya ba byung bar gyur te/pad mo chen 
9b2(phal chen): po de la de bzhin gshegs pa'i ri rab kyi rdul shin tu phra ba snyed de dag gi nang nas bskal pa'i thog ma pa de bzhin gshegs pa ye shes rin po che'i 'od kun tu 'phro ba'i dpal ston gyi rgyal po zhes bya ba byung bar gyur te de ni bskal pa de 
9b3(phal chen): la de dag thams cad kyi thog mar bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas te/des lo stong phrag du ma zhig du chos bstan pas/sems can yongs su smyin par mdzad nas/
9b4(phal chen): lo stong phrag bcur 'od gzer snang ba'i rnam par 'phrul pas yongs su smyin par mdzad/de bzhin gshegs pa de lo stong phrag bcu na 'byung bar 'gyur ro zhes bya ba'i tshe pad mo'i rgyal po chen po lo ma'i mtshams nas 
9b5(phal chen): du gsum gyi de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi yul rnam par ston pa'i 'od gzer gyi sprin gyi rgyan ces bya ba de las sems can thams cad rdul dang 'byed pa'i sgron ma zhes bya ba'i 'od gzer byung bar gyur te/'od des phog 
9b6(phal chen): pa'i sems can rnams kyis kyang lo stong phrag bcu na de bzhin gshegs pa 'byung bar 'gyur ro zhes bar shes so//lo stong phrag dgu na de bzhin gshegs pa de 'byung bar 'gyur ro zhes pa de 'byung bar 'gyur/byang chub kyi shing de 
9b7(phal chen): las rdul dang bral ba'i dpal gyi snying po zhes bya ba'i 'od gzer 'byung ste/'od gzer des phog pa'i sems can de dag gis kyang gzugs thams cad shin tu phra bar mthong bar gyur to//lo stong phrag brgyad na de bzhin 
9b8(phal chen): gshegs pa 'byung ngo zhes bya ba'i tshe/byang chub kyi shing chen po de nyid las 'od gzer sems can thams cad kyi las rnam par smyin pa'i dbyings zhes bya ba byung ste/des phog pa'i sems can dag thams cad kyis kyang rang 
10a1(phal chen): @#//rang gi las rgya mtsho khong du chud par gyur te/las dran pa'i ye shes kyang thob par gyur to//lo stong phrag bdun na de bzhin gshegs pa de 'byung bar 'gyur ro zhes bya ba'i tshe/byang chub kyi shing de nyid las 'od gzer 
10a2(phal chen): dge ba'i rtsa ba thams cad yang dag par 'byung ba'i dbyangs shes bya ba byung ste/'od gzer des sems can de la phog nas dbang po nyams pa med cing yongs su tshang bar gyur to//lo stong phrag drug na de bzhin gshegs pa 'byung 
10a3(phal chen): bar 'gyur ro zhes bya ba'i tsho/byang chub kyi shing chen po de nyid las 'od gzer sangs rgyas kyi yul bsam gyis myi khyab pa'i dbyangs shes bya ba byung ba ste/des sems can de dag la phog nas bsam pa rgya chen pos rnam par 'phrul 
10a4(phal chen): par gyur to//lo stong phragalnga na de bzhin gshegs pa da 'byung bar 'gyur ro zhes ba'i tshe/byang chub kyi shin chen po de nyid las 'od zer sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad yongs su dag par sgra 'byin pa'i gzugs brnyan 
10a5(phal chen): rnam par dmigs pa zhes bya ba byung ste/des sems can de da la phog nas sangs rgyas kyi zhing rnam pa thams cad du yongs su dag par mthong bar gyur to//lo stong prag bzhin de bzhin gshegs pa de 'byung bar 'gyur ro zhes
10a6(phal chen): bya ba'i tshe/byang chub kyi shing chen pa de nyid las 'od gzer de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi yul tha myi dad pa'i sgron ma zhes bya ba byung ste/de sems can de dag la phig nas de bzhin gshegs pa'i rnam par 'phrul pa 
10a7(phal chen): thams cad du song ba la zhugs par gyur to//lo stong phrad gsum na de bzhin gshegs pa de'abyung bar 'gyur ro zhes bya ba'i tshe/byang chub kyi shes chen po de las 'od gzer 'gro ba thams cad la mngon du gyur pa'i sgron ma 
10a8(phal chen): zhes bya ba byung ste/des sesams can de dag la phog nas de bzhin gshegs ba de yang mngon du mos pas mthong bar gyur to//la stong phrag gnyis na de bzhin gshegs pa da 'byung bar 'gyur ro zhes ba ba'i tshe/byang chub kyi shin chen 
10b1(phal chen): po de las 'od gzer dus gsum gyi ye shes kyi glog gi sgron ma zhes bya ba de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sngon gyi tshul rab tu sgrog pa byung ste/des sems can de dag la reg nas de bzhin gshegs pa de'i sngon gyi tshul rgya mtsho la 
10b2(phal chen): rtog cing 'jug par gyur to//lo stong na de bzhin gshegs pa de 'byung bar 'gyur ro zhes bya ba'i tshe/byang chub kyi shing chen po de las 'od gzer ye shes rab rib med pa/de bzhin gshegs pa'i sgron ma zhes bya ba byung ste 
10b3(phal chen): des sems can de dag la phog nas de bzhin gshegs par thams cad kyi rnam par 'phrul pa dang /zhing thams cad dang /sems can thams cad kyis mthong bar 'gyur ba'i myig kun tu lta ba thob par gyur to//de bzhin gshegs 
10b4(phal chen): pa de lo brgya na 'byung bar 'gyur ro//zhes bya ba'i tshe/byang chub gyi shing chen po de nyid las 'od gzer 'gro ba thams cad kyis sangs rgyas mthong ba rnam par smin pa'i dge ba'i rtsa ba las byung ba zhes bya ba byung ste/des sems 
10b5(phal chen): can de dag la phog nas/de bzhin gshegs pa'i skye ba'i 'du shes thob par gyur to//de bzhan gshegs pa de zhag bdun na skye bar 'gyur ro zhes bya ba'itshe/byang chub kyi shing chen pa de nyid las 'od gzer sems can thams cad 
10b6(phal chen): rab tu dga' ba dang /spro ba dang smos pa yongs su 'byung ba'i dbyangs shes bya ba byung ste/des sem can de dag la phog nas sangs rgyas mthong bar 'gyur ba'i dga' ba'i shugs chen po skyes par gyur to//rigs kyi bu de ltar 'od 
10b7(phal chen): gzer gyis yongs su smin par bya ba'i tshul tshad med pa rnam pa de ltar bus lo stong du sems can rnams yongs su smin par mdzad nas zhag bdun pa la thug pa dang /'jig rten gyi khams de thams cad yongs su bskyod pa'i tshul tshad 
10b8(phal chen): med pas yongs su bskyod nas shin tu dag par byin gyis brlabs so//phyogs bcu'i de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sangs rgyas kyi zhing yongs su dag pa ji snyed pa rgyan bsam gyis mi khyab pa rnam pa sna tshogs dang ldan pa 
11a1(phal chen): @#//de dag thams cad kyang sangs rgyas kyi zhing der sems kyi skad cig re re la yongs su bstan par gyur to//de nas zhag bdun pa tha mal bab pa dang /'jig rten gyi khams de na sems can sangs rgyas mthong bar 
11a2(phal chen): 'gyur bar yongs su smin pa de dag thams cad snying po byang chub tu mngon par gzhol par gyur to//de nas 'jig rten gyi khams de'i 'khor yug thams cad dang /ri rab thams cad dang /ri thams cad dang /chu klung thams 
11a3(phal chen): cad dang /rgya mtsho thams cad dang /shing thams cad dang /sa'i gzhi thams cad dang /grong khyer thams cad dang /ra ba thams cad dang /gnas thams cad dang /gzhal med khang thams cad dang /gos dang rgyan dang /
11a4(phal chen): longs spyod thams cad dang /rol mo'i shing thams cad dang /rol mo brdung ba'i dbyangs thams cad dang /sbrul pa'i rgyan thams cad kyi dmigs pa re re las kyang de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi yul dang /mthu'i sgra rab 
11a5(phal chen): tu sgrog pa'i spos dang /bdug spos thams cad kyi sprin mngon par 'thon te/rin po che'i 'od 'phro pa'i sprin dang /spos dang bdug spos thams cad kyi'od 'phro ba dam pa dang /spos rin po che thams cad kyi gzugs kyi sprin 
11a6(phal chen): dang /gos rin po che dang /rgyan rin po che thams cad kyis sprin dang /rin po che'i me tog sna tshogs kyi ri rab kyi sprin dang phye ma sna tshogs kyi sprin mang po de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad kyi 'od gzer gyi sprin rnam par 
11a7(phal chen): ston pa de bzhin gshegs pa thamasas cad kyi 'od kyi dkyil 'khor gyi sprin rab tu 'gyed pa/rol mo brdung pa dang /dkrol ba thams cad kyi sprin yongs su 'khrol zhing rdud pa/de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi 
11a8(phal chen): smon lam gyi sgra skad kyi sprin rab tu 'byin pa/de bzhin gshegs pa rnams cad kyi dbyangs kyi yan lag dang /gsung rgya mtsho'i sprin rab tu sgrog pa/de bzhin gshegs pa mthams cad kyi mtshan dang dpe byad bzang po'i 
11b1(phal chen): gzugs brnyan sna tshogs kyi sprin rab tu ston par byed cing /de bzhin gshegs pa'i ltas sngon du byung ba bsam gyis myi khyab pa yongs su ston te/rin po che'i rgyal po'i pad mo'i chen po'i lo ma'i mtshams nas/dus gsum gyi de bzhin gshegs 
11b2(phal chen): pa thams cad kyi yul rnam par ston pa'i 'od gzer gyi sprin gyi rgyan de'i pad mo'i 'khor rin po che'i rgyal po las byas pa chen po sha stag tu byas te 'khod do//rin po che'i rgyal po pad mo chen po'i 'khor gyi rin po che'i pad mo chen po de dag gi 
11b3(phal chen): ze pa dang /snying po la seng ge'i khri rin po che'i bang mo chen po'i snying po can/sangs rgyas kyi zhing bcu'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed dag byung bar gyur te/rin po che'i snying po can gyi khri de dag la'ang byang chub sems dpa' sangs rgyas kyi zhing bcu'i 
11b4(phal chen): rdul shin tu phra ba snyed skyil mo krung bcas te/'khor bar gyur to//bcom ldan 'das ye shes rin po che'i 'od kun tu 'phro ba'i dpal yon tan dpal gyi rgyal po de bzhin gshegs pa de bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu 
11b5(phal chen): mngon par sangs rgyas ma thag tu de'i mod la/phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams su de bzhin gshegs pa mang po dag kyang bla na med pa yang dag pa rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu mngon par sangs rgyas te/sems can rnams kyi 
11b6(phal chen): bsam pa ji lta ba bzhin du mngon sum du chos kyi 'khor lo skor bar gyur to//de nas kyang 'jig rten gyi khams de dag gi sems can tshang med pa rnams ngan song dang log par ltung ba thams cad las rnam par bzlog go/
11b7(phal chen): sems can tshad med pa ni mtho ris su skye ba la rab tu bkod do//sems can tshad med pa ni nyan thos la rab tu bkod do//sem can tshad med pa ni rang sangs rgyas kyi byang chub tu yongs su smin par byas so//sems 
11b8(phal chen): can tshad med pa ni shugs drag po'i 'od kyis 'byung ba'i byang chub tu yongs su smi par byas so//sems can tshad med par ni brtson 'grus dri ma med pa'i rgyal mtshan gyi byang chub tu yongs su smyin par byas so//sems cad tshad med pa'i ni chos rab tu 
12a1(phal chen): @#//bsgom pa'i gnas su 'byung ba'i byang chub la rab tu bkod do//sems can tshad med pa ni dbang po yongs su dag pa rab tu bsgom pas byung ba'i byang chub tu yongs su smyin par byas so//sems can tshad med 
12a2(phal chen): pa ni stobs mnyam pa nyid la yang dag par spyod pa khong du chud pas 'byung ba'i byang chub du yongs su smyin par byas/sems can tshad med pa ni/chos kyi grong khyer mngon du 'gyur ba'i yul gyi theg pa skye bas 'byung ba'i byang chub 
12a3(phal chen): la rab tu bkod do//sem can tshad med pa ni thams cad du rjes su song ba'i rdzu 'phrul tha mi dad pa'i tshul gyis theg pas 'byung ba'i byang chub la bar tu bkod do//sems cad tshad med pa ni spyod pa'i sbyor ba la yang dag 
12a4(phal chen): par gzhol ba'i tshul gyis 'byung ba'i byang chub las rab tu bkod do//semasacan tshad med pa ni ting nge 'dzin gyis 'jug pa'i tshul gyis 'byung ba'i byangs chub la rab tu bkod do//sems can tshad med pa ni dmigs pa'i

12a6(phal chen): dpa'i lam la rab tu bkod do/sems can tshad med pa ni/pha rol tu phyin pa'i lam rnam par dag pa la rab tu bkod do//seMms can tshad med pa ni byang chub sems dpa' sa dang po la rab tu bkod do//ses can 
12a7(phal chen): tshad med pa ni byang chub kyi sems dpa'i sa gnyis pa dang /gsum pa dang bzhi pa dang lnga pa dang /drug pa dang bdun pa dang /brgyad pa dang /dgu pa dang la rab tu bkod do//se mas can tshad med pa ni bang chub sems dpa'i 
12a8(phal chen): sa bcu pa la rab tu bkod do//sems can tshad med par ni byang chub sems dpa'ispyod pa smon lam gyis khyad par can la btsud do//sems can tshad med pa ni byams chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang po'i spyod pa dang /smon lam rnam 
12b1(phal chen): par bkod pa la rab tu bkod do//de ltar de bzhin gshegs pa des sangs rgyas kyi khyu mchog gi mthu phyung ba dang /rnam par 'phrul pa bsam gyis mi khyab pas chos kyi 'khor lo bskor bas sems kyi skad cig re re la sems 
12b2(phal chen): can gyi khams mtha' dang dbus med pa rnam par dul bar gyur to//'jig rten gyi khams de'i sems can thams cad kyis kyang de bzhin gshegs pa de'i sku sna tshogs dang /thabs mkhas pa sna tshogs kyis bsgrubs pa'i chos 
12b3(phal chen): bstan pa yongs su shes par gyur te//rgyal po'i pho brang rin po che'i me tog sgron ma'i sems can gzugs dang /kha dog dang yul dang longs spyod kyis rgyags shing dregs te gcig la gcig khyad du gsod pa de rnams gdul ba'i 
12b4(phal chen): don du byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang pos kha dog dang gzugs mchog tu phyin par mngon par sprul te/rgyal po'i pho brang der phyin te/de'i 'od shin tu yangs pas rgyal po'i gnas de dag thams cad du yongs su snang bar gyur te/
12b5(phal chen): rgyal po'i gnas de'i 'od dang /'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal po rnam par snang ba rin chen pad mo dpal gyi gtsug phung snying po'i lus las byung ba'i 'od dang /bud med rin po che'i 'od dang /rin po che'i shing gi 'od dang /nor bu 
12b6(phal chen): rin po che chen po'i 'od dang /nyi zla dang gzA skar dang rgyu skar gyi 'od dang /'dzam bu'i gling na 'od ji snyed cig yod pa de dag thams cad kyang mi snang bar gyur te/dper na nyi ma shar te mun pa dang bral ba dang /zla ba dang gza' 
12b7(phal chen): skar dang /rgyu skar gyi 'od kyang mi snang la me dang nor bu'i 'od kyang mi snang ngo //de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang po'i zil gyis non na 'dzam bu'i gling de na 'od yod pa thams cad kyang mi snang par gyur to//'di lta ste 
12b8(phal chen): dper na/'dzam bu chu klung gi gser gyis le sbram gyi drung du snag tsa'i yul ba bzhag na mdzes par mi rung /lha ger mi rung /lham mer mi rung /lhang nger mi rung ngo //de bzhin du byangs chub sems dpa' kun tu gzang po'i mdun 
13a1(phal chen): @#//du sems can de dag gi gzugs kyi lus kyang mdzes par ma rung /lhag ger ma rung /lham mer ma rung /lhang nger ma rung ngo//de nas de dag 'di snyam du gyur te/de lta bu su zhig de ltar de'i drung du bdag cag gi lus 
13a2(phal chen): sam/'od dam kha dog gam/gzi brjid kyis kyang mdzes par ma rung/lhag ger ma rung /lhas mer ma rung /lhang nger ra rung ba de lta lha 'am/tshangs pa 'am/ci zhig de'i mtshams ni gzung bar myi nus so snyam mo//
13a3(phal chen): de nas byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang pos rgyal po'i gnas rin po che'i me tog sgron ma'i dbus 'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal po rnam par snang ba rin chen pad mo dpal gyi gtsug phud snying po'i gzhal med khang gi steng gi bar snang 
13a4(phal chen): la 'dug ste/'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal bo rnam par snang ba pad mo dpal gyi gtsug phad snying po la 'di skad ces smras so//rgyal po chen po shes par gyis shig/de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs 
13a5(phal chen): pa'i sangs rgyas 'jig rten du byung ste/khyod kyi khams 'di nyid kyi snying po byang chub chos kun tu snang ba'i chos kyi sprin gyi sgra dbyangs rgyal mtshan 'di nyid na rnam par spyod do zhes pa dang /rgyal po'i bu mo ye shes kyi 'od kun tu 'phro ba 
13a6(phal chen): pad mo bzang po mig yid du 'ong ba'i dpal gyi zla bas/byang chub sems dpa' kunatu bzang po'i gzugs kyi lus dang /'od rnam par 'phrul ba dang rgyan mthong ste/dbyangs thos nas dga' ba dang /mos pa'i shugs chen po 
13a7(phal chen): skyes te/de'i tshe sems 'di snyam du bskyed do//bdag gi dge ba'i rtsa ba gang ci bsags pa des kyang bdag kyang lus 'di 'dra bar thob par shog shig/rgyan 'di 'dra ba dang /mtshan 'di 'dra ba dang /spyod lam 'di 'dra ba 
13a8(phal chen): dang /rdzu 'phrul 'di 'dra bar shog shig/ji ltar 'dis mtshan mo mun nag gi nang na sems can rnams la snang ba chen po bskyed de/sangs rgyas skye ba yongs su brjod pa de bzhin du bdag gis kyang sems can rnams 
13b1(phal chen): kyi mi shes pa'i mun pa bsal de/ye shes chen po'i snang ba bskyed par bya'o//gang dang gang du skyes pa thams cad du'ang dge ba'i bshes gnyen 'di dang mi 'bral bar shog shig/de nas rigs kyi bu 'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal 
13b2(phal chen): po rnam par snang ba rin chen pad mo dpal gyi gtsug phud snying po yan lag bzhi'i dpung gi tshogs dang lhan cig/rin po che bdun dang lhan cig/bud med kyi tshogs kyi 'khor dang lhan cig/bu dang blon po dang /sdongs kyi mi dang 
13b3(phal chen): lhan cig du rgyal po'i rdzu 'phrul chen po dang /rgyal po'i mthu chen pos rgyal po'i gnas rin po che'i me tog sgron ma de nas 'phags te gyen tu nam mkha' la dpag tshad gcig tsam du mngon par 'phags nas/'dzam bu'i gling thams 
13b4(phal chen): cad dang /gling bzhi'i 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad snang ba chen pos rgyas par bkang ste/sems can thams cad sangs rgyas pa lta ba la yongs su bskul ba'i don du rin po che'i ri thams cad la gzugs brnyan mngon par 
13b5(phal chen): bstan cing gling bzhi pa'i 'jig rten gyi khams su gtogs pa'i sems can rnams la mngon sum du gnas te sangs rgyas la blta ba des tshigs su bcad de glu'i dbyangs kyis bsngags pa//lus can thams cad skyabs mdzad 
13b6(phal chen): pa'i//sangs rgyas 'jig rten byung bar gyur//'jig rten gtso bo de blta bar//kun kyang longs te 'dod par gyis//lus can kun la phan don du//chos rab ston par gang mdzad pa'i//de bzhin gshegs 
13b7(phal chen): pa 'byung ba ni//bskal pa bye ba'i brgya lam na'o//'jig rten phyin ci log gyur cing //mi shes bsgribs pas bsgribs gyur te//'khor ba'i sdug bsngal myong ba'i rnams//gzugs shing thugs rje che bskyed nas//

14b1(phal chen): kyi khri la rin po che'i gdan 'brel par bting ba'i sprin gyi rgyan nam mkha'i dbyings la byin gyis brlab pa dang /rin po che thams cad kyi rgyan mtshan gyi sprin mngon par bsgreng ba'i rgyan nam mkha'i dbyings la/byin gyis brlab pa dang /khang pa dang 
14b2(phal chen): gzhal med khang thams cad kyi sprin gyis kun nas yog pa'i rgyan nam mkha'i dbyings pa byin gyis brlab pa dang me tog dang tshogs kyis kun nas yog pa'i rgyan ni nam mkha'i dbyings la byis gyis brlab pa dang /mchod pa'i rgyan thams 
14b3(phal chen): cad kyi sprin gyis char rab tu 'bab pa'i rgyan nam mkha'i dbying la byin gyis brlab pa byed cing /bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa ye shes rin po che'i 'od kun tu 'phro ba'i dpal yon tan dpal gyi rgyal po gang na ba der song 
14b4(phal chen): ste/phyin nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin bshegs pa'i ye shes rin po che'i 'od du kun tu 'phro ba'i dpal/yon tan dpal gyi rgyal bo'i zhabs gnyis la spyi bos phyag 'tshal te/bcom ldan 'das la lan brgya stong du mar g.yas 
14b5(phal chen): phyogs su bskor ba byas nas/bcom ldan 'das kyi spyan sngar stan nor rin po che chen po'i phyogs kun tu rnam par snang ba'i pad mo'i snying po can la 'dug go/de nas 'khor los sgyur ba'i bu mo ye shes rin po che'i 'od 
14b6(phal chen): du 'phro ba'i pad mo bzang mo mig yid du 'ong ba'i dpal gyi zla ba'i dkyil 'khor gyis lus las 'grol te/bcom ldan 'das de bzhi gshegs pa ye shes kyi 'od kun tu 'phro ba'i dbal yon tan dpal gyi rgyal 
14b7(phal chen): po la gtor to//mngon par gtor to//de nas rgyan de dag bcom ldan 'das de'i dbu'i gtsug gi steng gi bar snang la nor bu rin po che'i rgyun gyi gdugs chen po nor bu rin po che sna tshogs kyi dra bas yongs su bskor ba klu'i dbang po'i lus kyis gzung ba/
14b8(phal chen): rgyan thams cad kyis lus kyis dbyibs shin tu legs par kun nas bskor ba/rgyan gyi gdugs kyi dkyil 'khor bcus yongs su bskor ba/shin tu dag pa khang pa brtsegs pa'i dbyibs sna tshogs kyis brgyan pa/rin po che 
15a1(phal chen): che thams cad kyi lhab lhub kyi sprin gyis kun nas yog pa/nor bu rin po che'i rgyal po thams cad kyi shing gi rgyan gyis kun nas khebs pa/nor bu rin po che'i rkyal po spos thams cad kyi rgya mtshos yongs su brgyan 
15a2(phal chen): par 'dug par gyur te/de'i dbus na byang chub kyi shing chen po chos kyi dbyings las byung ba'i rin po che thams cad kyi yan lag dang /lhun du ldan pa zhes bya ba rgyan pa'i brjod na mtha' dang dbus med pa skad cig re re zhing rgyan 
15a3(phal chen): sna tshogs yongs su ston pa/yod pa mthong bar gyur to//de'i drung na de bzhin gshegs pa rnam par snang mdzad ces bya ba byang chub sems dpa' sangs rgyas kyi zhing brjod du med pa'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed kun kyang byang 
15a4(phal chen): chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang po'i spyod pa dang /smon lam gyis byung ba byang chub sems dpa' rgyan sna tshogs tha mi dad cing bsam gyis mi khyab pa la rnam par spyod pa sha stag gis bskor cing mdun du byas pa dang /'jig rten 
15a5(phal chen): gyi dbang po thams cad kyang dol mngon du gyur mthong ngo //bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad de'i sangs rgyas kyi rnam par 'phrul ba mtha' dang dbus med pa'ang mthong bar gyur to//sngon gyi byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa 
15a6(phal chen): bskal pa gcig nas gcig tu rgyud pa la'ang zhugs par gyur to//'jig rten gyi khams de'i 'du ba dang 'jig pa'i bskal pa'ang zhugs par gyur to/'jig rten gyi khams der 'das pa'i sangs rgyas kyi rgyud gcig nas

15a8(phal chen): sems can thams cad yongs su smin par bya ba dang /gdul bar bya ba la mngon du gyur pa yang mthong ngo //byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyang byang chub sems dpa' kunatu bzang po'i lus la gzugs brnyan du gyur

16a1(phal chen): skad sna tshogs rgya mtsho shin tu bstan pa dang ldan pa dang /mdo sde'i sprin sna tshogs rjes su sgrog pa can dag kyang mthong bar gyur to//mthong nas de bas kyang dga' ba dang mos pa'i shugs chen po thob par gyur 
16a2(phal chen): te/de de ltar dga' ba'i shugs chen po skyes par gyur pa la/bcom ldan 'das ye shes rin po che'i 'od kun tu 'phro ba'i dpal yon tan dpal gyi rgyal po de bzhin gshegs pa des de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi chos kyi 
16a3(phal chen): 'khor lo rab tu bsgrags pa zhes bya ba'i mdo sde 'khor gyi mdo sde/sangs rgyas kyi zhing bcu'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed dang bcas pa yang dag par bstan te/des mdo sde de thos par gyur nas/ting nge 'dzin gyi sgo stong phrag bcu

16a6(phal chen): du rnam par dmigs pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin dang /zhing 'byam klas pa thams cad du snang ba rjes su song ba zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzinadang /dus gsum gyi tshul thams cad la 'jug pa khong du chud pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dz-in

18a5(phal chen): tshe de'i dus na rgyal po'i btsun mo'i dpal gyi bzhin yongs su rgyas pa zhes bya ba byung ba de/gzhan zhig yin snyam du sems na yang de ltar mi blta ste/mtshan mo'i lha mo kho mo'i drung na 'dug pa sgra rgya mtsho rab tu zhi ba dang ldan pa 'di yin no//
18a6(phal chen): rigs kyi bu de la ji snyam tu sems/de'i tshe de'i dus ni rgyal po'i bu mo ye shes kyi 'od kun tu 'phro ba/pad mo bzang mo mig yid du 'ong ba'i dpal gyi zla ba zhes bya ba de gzhan zhig yin snyams du sems na ni de ltar mi blta ste/kho mo ni de'i tshe de'i dus 
18a7(phal chen): na rgyal po'i bu mo ye shes gyi 'od kun tu 'phro ba/bad mo bzang mo mig yid ngu 'ong ba'i dpal gyi zla bzhes bya bar gyur to//kho mo'i bu mo gzho nur gyur to//de bzhin gshegs pa dbang po'i rgyal mtshan dpal gyi gsung rab la/de bzhin 
18a8(phal chen): gshegs pa'i sku pad mo la bzhugs pa bcos pa de ni kho mo bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub kyi rgyur gyur to//de'i byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang pos/kho mo bla na med pa yang dad par rdzogs pa'i byang chub
18b1(phal chen): tu yongs su bskul ba de ni/kho mos byang chub tu sems dang po bskyed pa yin no//kho mos bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa ye shes rin po che'i 'od kun tu 'phro ba'i dpal yon tan dpal gyi rgyal po'i drung du phyin te/
18b2(phal chen): rgyan gyis gtor nas de bzhin gshegs pa'i rnam par 'phrul pa dang /cho 'phrul mthong bar gyur te/kho mos bcom ldan 'das de'i drung nas chos thos par gyur pa de'i tshe/kho mos byang chub sems dpa'i rnam par thar pa/'jig

18b4(phal chen): cad kyis kyang bkur stir byas so//de bzhin gshegs pa de dag gis chos bstan pa de dag thams cad kyang kho mos thos te/de bzhin gshegs pa de dgag gi gdams ngag dang rjes su bstan pa la'ang zhugs par gyur to//
18b5(phal chen): de bzhin gshegs pa de dgal kho mos rim gror cher byas te/rim gro de'i rnam pas sems kyi skad cig gcig la de bzhin gshegs pa de dang thams cad dang /byang chub sems dpa'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor de dag dang /

18b7(phal chen): rin chen 'khor lo sna tshogs kyis klubs shing brgyan pa zhes bya ba/bskal pa ni 'od chen po zhes bya ba zhig byung ste/de la sangs rgyas lnga brgya byung bar gyur to//de dag thams cad kyang kho mas bsnyen bkur te/bskal pa 'od chen po 
18b8(phal chen): de la de bzhin gshegs pa thugs rje chen po'i sprin gyi rgyal mtshan zhes bya ba bskal pa'i dang po las grub ste/de ma 'o na bar byung ba'i tshe/kho mo mtshan mo'i lha mor gyur pas mchod pa byas so//de'i 'og tu de bzhin gshegs pa rdo rje

19a2(phal chen): rdul shin tu phra ba snyed kyi 'khor dang bcas pa yang dag par bstan te/de yang kho mos thos nas yongs su bzung ngo //de'i 'og tu de bzhin gshegs pa me'i 'od 'phro ba ri'i dpal gyis rnam par brgyan pa zhes bya ba byung ste/de la/kho mo

19a4(phal chen): bstan te/de yang kho mor thos nas yongs su bzung do//de'i 'og tu de bzhin bshegs pa chos rgya mtsho thams cad mngon par 'phags pa'i shugs kyi rgyal po zhes bya ba byung ste/de kha mo lha'i rgyal por gyur pas mchod pa

19a6(phal chen): de'i 'og tu de bzhin gshegs pa chos zab mo'i dpal rgya mtshos yang dag byung ba 'od ces bya ba byung ste/de la kho mo klu'i dbang po'i bu mor gyur pas/nor bu rin po che yid bzhin gyi rgyal po'i sprin gyi char mngon par phab ste
19a7(phal chen): mchod pa byas so//de bzhin gsheg pa des kho mo la mdo sde dga' ba rgya mtsho rnam par 'phel ba'i shugs shes bya ba mdo sde bye ba brgya stong phrag bcu'i 'khor dang bcas pa yanadag par bstan te/de yang kho mor thos nas
19a8(phal chen): yongs su bzung ngo//de'i 'og tu de bzhin gsheg pa rin chen ri bo spo'i 'od gzer sgron ma zhes bya ba byung ste/de la kha mo rgya mtsho'i lha mor gyur pas/rin po che'i pad mo'i sprin gyi char gyis nye bar bsnyen te mchod pa byas so//
19b1(phal chen): de bzhin gshegs pa des kho mo la mdo sde chos dbyings rgya mtsho'i tshul zhes bya ba mdo sde sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi rdul shin tu phra ba snyed kyi 'khor dang bcas pa yang dag par bstan te/de'ang kho mos thos nas yongs su blangs so//
19b2(phal chen): dran pas yang dag par bzung ngo //de'i 'og tu de bzhin gshegs pa yon tan rgya mtsho snang ba'i dkyil 'khor gyi dpal zhes bya ba byung ste/de la kho mo drang srong mngon par shes pa lnga dang ldan par gyur pa/drang srong stong phrag drug bcus 
19b3(phal chen): kun nas bskor bas rdzu 'phrul chen po'i rnam par 'phrul pas nye bar bstan te/spos kyi me tog ri bo tsam gyi sprin gyi char mngon par phab ste/mchod pa byas so//de bzhin gshegs pa des khom la mdo sde chos gnas med 
19b4(phal chen): pa'i sgron ma zhes bya ba mdo sde stong phrag bcu'i 'khor dang bcas par bstan te/de yang kho mos thos nas yongs gzung ngo //de'i 'og tu de'i bzhin gshegs pa rnam par snang mdzad dpal gyi snying po zhes bya ba byung ste/kho mo yang 
19b5(phal chen): mnyam pa nyid kyi don 'byung ba zhes bya ba'i sa'i lha mo zhig tu gyur nas/kho mo sa'i lha mo'i 'khor grangs med pa dang bcas pas rin po che'i shing thams cad kyi lhun las rin po che'i me tog gi sprin gyi char mngon par bab ste/
19b6(phal chen): rin po che'i phreng ba'i sprin gyi char yang rab tu phab nas/de bzhin gshegs pa de la mchod pa bya ba'i phyir spyan sngar phyin to//de bzhin gshegs pa de'i kho mo la mdo sde de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad yang dag par 'byung ba'i gnas snying 
19b7(phal chen): po zhes bya ba'i mdo sde grangs med pa'i 'khor dang bcas pa yang dag par bstan te/de yang kha mos thos nas bzung ste chud zos par ma byas so//rigs kyi bu de bzhin gshegs pa lnga brgya po de dag thams cad kyi tha mar de bzhin 
19b8(phal chen): gshegs pa chos kyi dbyings nam mkha' mdzod spus yongs su rgyas pa'i rtse mo dpal gyi sgron ma zhes bya ba 'jig rten du byung ste/de'i tshe kho mo yang gar mkhan gyi bu mo bzhin mngon par mdzes pa'i dpal zhes bya ba zhig tu gyur te/

20a2(phal chen): gshegs pa de la mngon par bstod de spyan sngar phyin to//de bzhin gshegs pa des mdzod sbu nas 'od gzer chos kyi dbyings rnam par snang ba'i rgyan ces bya ba bkye ste/kho mo'i lus thams cad kyang snang bar mdzad do//

20a6(phal chen): 'gro ba'i tshe yang /sangs rgyas kyi chos yongs su brjod par bya bas sems can tshad med pa'i don byas so//de bzhin gshegs pa dedag re re yang kho mos chos kyi dbyings kyi rang bzhin rgya mtsho dus gsum gyi ye shes

20b1(phal chen): ba sngan chad ma mthong ba dag kyang kho mo'i sems kyi rgyud la gnas par gyur to//'on kyang byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang po'i spyod pa las g.yo ba med do//de ci'i phyir zhe na/thams cad mkhyen pa'i glog mngon par snang ba

23b2(phal chen): cad kyi yid mi 'ong ba'i gzugs dang/sgra dang dri dang ro dang reg yongs su rgyu pa rnam par bzlog pa la zhugs pa'o/sems can thams cad kyi sdug pa dang mi sdug pa dang bral zhing phrad pa'i sdug bsngal rab tu zhi bar bya ba la
23b3(phal chen): zhugs pa'o//sems can thabs cad kyi yul gyi rkyen las byung bas mgo rmongs pa'i sdug bsngal rnam par bzlog pa la rab tu brtson pa'o//log par ltung ba'i sems can thams cad kyi rten du gyur pa'o//
23b4(phal chen): sems can thams cad 'khor ba'i gnas kyi sdug bsngal las 'byung pa yang dag par ston pa la mngon par brtson pa 'o//sems can thams can kyi rga shi dang mya ngan dang smre sngags 'don pa dang/sdug bsngal dang
23b5(phal chen): yid mi bde ba dang/'khrug pa rnam par bzlog pa lazhugs pa'o//sems can thams cad la de bzhin gshegs pa'i bde ba bla na myed pa yongs su bsgrub par zhugs pa ste/grong dang grong khyer dang/ljongs dang
23b6(phal chen): yul dang khams dang/rgyal po'i gnas na 'khod pa'i sems can thams cad bde bar rim gror bya bas nyams dga' bar 'gyur zhing de dag la chos dang 'thun par bsrung ba dang/bskyab pa dang rim gror bya ba yang
23b7(phal chen): legs par khyed de/de dag rim pas thams cad mkhyen pa nyid du yongs su smin par byed do//'di lta ste khang khyim dang/gzhal med khang na gnas pa'i sems can rnams kyi mngon par chags pa yongasu skyed
23b8(phal chen): do//yid mi bde ba dang de dag kyang rnam par sel to/mngon par zhen pa thams cad rgyun gcad pa dang/chos thams cad kyi rang bzhin yongs su shes par bya ba'i phyir chos ston to//pha ma dang phu nu dang srid me
24a1(phal chen): @#//dang gnyen bshes dang 'grogs pa'i sems can yun ring po nas bshes shing 'dza' bar gyur pa dag la sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' dang phrad par bya ba'i phyir chos ston to//bu smad dang 
24a2(phal chen): 'grogs pa'i sems can rnams la 'khor ba'i sred pa thams cad rab tu spang ba dang /sems can thams cad la mnyam pa nyid kyi sems su bya ba dang /snying rje chen po rab tu thob par bya ba'i phyir chos stonato//
24a3(phal chen): tshor dus kyi dbus na 'khod pa'i sems can rnams las 'phags pa'i dge 'dun dang de bzhin gshegs pa mthong bar bya ba la yang dag par gzhol bar bya ba'i phyir chos ston te//longs spyod kyi dregs pas mos pa'i sems can 
24a4(phal chen): rnams la bzod pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa yongs su rdzogs par bya ba'i phyir chos stonate//glu dang gar dang rol mo la mngon par dga' ba'i sems can rnams la chos kyi dga' ba la mos par bya ba'i phyir chos sten to//
24a5(phal chen): yul gyi dga' ba la chags pa'i sems can rnams de bzhin gshegs pa'i yul la yang dag par gzholabar bya ba'i phyir chos stonato//khro bas yongs su non pa'i sems can rnams la bzod pa'i pha ral tu phyin pa la 
24a6(phal chen): rab tu dgod par bya ba'i phyir chos sto to//sems can le lo can rnams la brtson 'grus kyi pha rol tu phyin pa yongs du dag par bya ba'i phyir chos stong to//sems rnam par mos pa'i sems can rnams la
24a7(phal chen): de bzhin gshegs pa'i bsam gtan gyi pha rol tu phyin pa rab tu thob par bya ba'i phyir chos stod to//lta ba'i rnam pa'i thibs po'i subs pa'i sems can ma rig pa'i mun nag na 'thom pa rnams la lta ba'i rnam pa thibs po dang 
24a8(phal chen): ma rig pa'i mun pa rnam par bstsal ba'i phyir chos stonato//shes rab 'chal pa can gyi sems can rnams la shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa rab du thob par bya ba'i phyir chosastonato//khams gsum pa la mngon par
24b1(phal chen): zhen pa'i sems can rnams la 'khor ba las 'byung bar bya ba'i phyir chos ston to//dma' ba la mos pa'i sems can rnams la de bzhin gshegs pa'i byang chub tu smon lam yongs su rdzogs par bya ba'i phyir chos ston to //bdag 
24b2(phal chen): la phan par brtson pa'i sems can rnams lasems can thams cad la phan par bya ba'i smon lam yongs su rdzogasapar 'gyur bar bya ba'i phyir chos stonato//bsam pa nyam chung ba'i sems can rnams 
24b3(phal chen): la byang chub sems dpa'i stobs kyi pha rol tu phyin pa rnam par dag par bya ba'i phyir chos stonato /ma rig pa'i mun pa'i sems blong ba'i sems can rnams la byang chub sems dpa'i ye shes kyi pha rol tu phyin pa rna par
24b4(phal chen): bya ba'i phyir chos ston to//gzugs mi sdug pa dang ldan pa'i sems can rnams ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i gzugs rnam par dag par bya ba 'i phyir chos stonato//lus kyi dbyibs mi mdzes pa dang ldan pa'i sems
24b5(phal chen): can rnams la chos kyi sku bla na med pa rnam par dag pa'i phyir chos stonato/kha dog mi sdug pa dang ldan pa'isems can rnams la de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku mdog gser gyi kha dog lta bu dang shin tu 'jam zhing ka tsa li 'da'i
24b6(phal chen): ltar reg na bde ba'i lus rnam par dag pa'i phyir tshos stonato//sdug bsngal ba'i sems can rnams lade bzhin gshegs pa'i bden pa dam ba thob bar bya ba'i phyir chos ston to//bde ba'i sems can rnams lathams
24b7(phal chen): cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi bde ba rab tu thob par bya ba'i phyir chos stonato/nad ba na ba'i sems can rnams la byang chub sems dpa'i lus gzugs brnyan lta bus yongs su grub par bya ba'i phyir chos stanato//dga' ba sna
24b8(phal chen): tshogs la brtson pa'i sems can rnams la bya chub sems dpa'i spyod pa'i dga' ba rab tu thob par bya pa'i phyir chos stonato//phongs pa'i sems can rnams labyang chub sems dpa'i chos kyi gter dang mdzod rab tu
25a1(phal chen): @#//thob par bya ba'i phyir chos ston to/bza' shing gi rab na gnas pa'i sems can rnams la sangs rgyas kyi chos yongs su btsal bar la mngon par brtson pa'i rgyu thob par bya ba'i phyir chos stan to/
25a2(phal chen): lam du zhugs pa'i sems can rnams thams cad mkhyen pa'i lam du 'jug par bya ba'i phyir chos stonato//grong na gnas pa'i sems can dang rnams la khams gsum pa thams cad 'byung bar bya ba'i phyir chos ston te 
25a3(phal chen): yul gyi mngab na gnas pa'i sems cad rnams la nyan thos dang rang byang chub kyi lam las yang dag par 'da' bar bya ba dang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i sa la rab tu dgong par bya ba'i phyir chos stong to//grong rdal
25a4(phal chen): na 'khod pa'i sems can rnams la chos kyi rgyal po'i pho brang du gnas par 'gyur bar bya ba'i phyir chos stod to//phyogs mtshams na 'khod pa'i sems can rnal la dus gsum mnyam pa nyid kyi ye shes rab tu thob 
25a5(phal chen): par bya ba'i phyir chos ston to//phyogs na gnas pa'i sems can rnam la chos thams cad ye shes mngon par shes par bya ba'i phyir chos sto to/'dod chags la gcig tu dga' ba'i sems can rnams la mi stug pa'i sgos 
25a6(phal chen): 'khor ba'i sred pa rnam par bzlog pa'i phyir chos ston to//zhe sdang la spyod pa'i sems can rnams pa byams pa chen po'i tshul rgya mtsho la 'jug par bya ba'i phyir chos stonato//gti mug la spyod pa'i sems can
25a7(phal chen): rnams la chos thams cad kyi sgo rgya mtsho rab tu 'byed pa'i ye shes mngon par shes bar bya ba'i phyir chos stonato //cha mnyam pa la spyod pa'i sems can nams la theg pa thams cad kyi smon lam gyi tshul rgya mtshos khyad par
25a8(phal chen): du 'gro bar bya ba'i phyir chos stong to//'khor ba'i yul la dga' ba'i bsam pa can gyi sems can rnams la 'khor ba'i yul la dga' ba rnam par bzlog pa'i phyi chos ston to//'khor ba'i sdug bsngal
25b1(phal chen): thams cad kyis gces pa'i sems can rnams la 'khor ba'i sdug bsngal thams cad kyis nye bar nyon mongs pa med par bya ba'i phyir chos ston to//de bzhin gshegs pas 'dul ba'i sems can rnams la mi skye ba'i 
25b2(phal chen): gnas yang dag par bstan pa'i phyir chos ston to//phung po la gnas pa'i bsam pa can gyi sems can rnams la gzhi med pa'i chos kyi spyod yul la rnam par spyod par bya ba'i phyir chos stong to/bsam pa zhum pa'i sems 
25b3(phal chen): can rnams la lam gyi rgyan gyi khyad par yang dag par bstan par bya ba'i phyir chos stonato//mtho ba'i nga rgyal can gyi sems can rnamasal chos thams cad mnyam pa nyid kyi bzod pa yang dag par bstan pa'i phir 
25b4(phal chen): chos ston to//g.yo sgyu'i bsam pa dang ldan pa'i sems can rnams la byang chub sems dpa'i bsam pa yongs su dag par bya ba'i phyir chos stonate/de ltar rigs kyi bu kho mo ni sems can thams cad chos 
25b5(phal chen): kyi sbyin pas yongs su bsdus shing ngan song gi lam gyi rgyud sdug bsngal thams cad nas rnam par bzlog pa dang /lha dang mi'i phun sum tshogs pa dang bde ba yongs su bstan pa dang/khams gsum pa las gyen du bskyod 
25b6(phal chen): pa dang /thams cad mkhyen pa nyid la rab tu dgod pa dang /thabs rnam pa sna tshogs kyis yongs su smin par byed byed pas/dga' ba chen po'i shugs rgya mtsho snang ba rab tu thob pas dga' zhing spro la yid bde bar 
25b7(phal chen): gyur to//gzhan yang rigs kyi bu kho mo ni phyogs dang phyogs mtshams kyi byang chub sems dpa'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor rgya mtsho la rnam par lta zhing smon lam rgya mtsho la spyod pa'i byang chub sems dpa' lus 
25b8(phal chen): sna tshogs rnam par dag pa/'od kyi dkyil 'khor sna tshogs kyi rgyan dang ldan pa/'od gzer dang 'od kyi dkyil 'khor kha dog mtha' yas pa rab tu 'gyed pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi tshul rgya mtshos tshogs pa'i ye 
26a1(phal chen): @#//shes snang ba can/tang nge 'dzin rgya mtsho sna tshogs la zhugs pa'i rnam par 'phrul pa sna tshogs kyi yul dang ldan pa/dbyangs kyi yan lag dang skad sna tshogs kyi dbyangs dang ldan pa/rgyan sna tshogs kyis rnam 
26a2(phal chen): par glubs pa'i lus can/de bzhin gshegs pa'i tshul sna tshogs la zhugs pa/zhing rgya mtsho 'byam klas pa sna tshogs su zhugs pa'i lus dang ldan pa/sangs rgyas rgya mtsho sna tshogs la zhugasapa/so so yang dag par rig pa 
26a3(phal chen): rgya mtsho sna tshogs la zhugs ba/de bzhin gshegs pa'i rnam par mdzad pa dang /ye shes kyi yul sna tshogs kyis snang bar gyur pa/ye shes rgya mtsho sna tshogs kyi snang ba rab tu thob pa/ting nge 'dzin rgya mtsho sna tshog sa kyi tshul 
26a4(phal chen): rnam par spyod pa/sna tshogs kyi rnam par thar pa'i gzhal med khang na rnam par rtse ba'i yul dang ldan pa/thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi sgo sna tshogs la mngon du gnas pa/chos kyi dbyings sna tshogs kyi nam mkha'i rgyan dang ldan 
26a5(phal chen): pa/rgyan sna tshogs kyi sprin gyis nam mkha' rgyas par 'gengs pa/'khor gyi dkyil 'khor rgya mtsho sna tshogs la rnam par lta ba/'jig rten gyi khams sna tshogs nas 'dus pa/sangs rgyas kyi zhing sna tshogs 'byam klas 
26a6(phal chen): par rjes su 'dod pa/phyogs rgya mtsho sna tshogs nas yongs su 'dus pa/de bzhin gshegs pa tha dad pas bkye ba/de bzhin gshegs ba tha dad pa'i zhabs kyi drung nas lhags pa/byang chub sems dpa'i tshogs sna 
26a7(phal chen): tshogs kyi 'khor dang ldan pa/rgyan sna tshogs kyi sprani gyi char rab tu 'bebs pa/de bzhin gshegasapa'i tshul sna tshogs la zhugs pa de bzhin gshegs pa'i chos rgya mtsho sna tshogs kyi 'khor dang ldan ba/
26a8(phal chen): ye shes sna tshogs rgya mtsho la zhugs pa/rgyan gyi snying po sna tshogs can gyi stan la 'khod pa rnams kyi dga' ba sna tshogs kyi shugs rgya mtsho yongs su bskyed de/de dag dga' ba sna tshogs kyi shu gas rgya mtsho yongs su bskye 
26b1(phal chen): nas/de bzhin gshegs pa'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor rgya mtshor 'dod ste/de dag de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs tshad med pa rjes su sems pas kyang dga' ba'i shugs chen po rgya mtsho yongs su 'byung bar 'gyur ro//
26b2(phal chen): rigs kyi bu gzhan yang kho mo ni bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyi gzugs kyi sku yongs su dag pa mtshan gyis rab tu brgyan pa/bsam gyis mi khyab pa mthong nas dga' ba chen po dang mos shing bde ba rab tu 
26b3(phal chen): thob ste/sems kyi skad cig re re la 'ang 'od kyi dkyil 'khor chos kyi dbyings ltar yangs pa/kha dog rgya mtsho mtha' dang dbus med par snang ba la lta zhing sems kyi skad cig re re las dga' ba chen po'i shugs rgya 
26b4(phal chen): mtsho rab tu 'thob po//rigs kyi bu gzhan yang bcom ldan 'das rnam pa snang mdzad kyi sku'i ba spu'i sgo re re nas kyang 'od gzer chen po sangs rgyas kyi zhing mtha' dang dbus med pa'i rdul shin tu phra 
26b5(phal chen): ba snyed byung ste/'od gzer re re zhing yang 'od gzer rgya mtsho sangs rgyas kyi mtha' yas pa'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed kyi 'khor dang ldan pas chos kyi dbyings thams cad rgyas par 'gengs pa dang /sems can thams 
26b6(phal chen): cad kyi sdug bsngal rab tu zhi bar mdzad pa mthong zhing sems kyi skad cig rere la dga' ba'i chen po'i ru las rgya mtsho rab tu 'thob po//rigs kyi bu gzhan yang bco ma ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyi dbu dang 
26b7(phal chen): sku phrag pa gnyis kyi lhun las sems kyi skad cig re rel rin po che'i thams cad kyi 'od 'phro ba'i ri'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad kyi rdul shin tu phra ba snyed 'byung zhing /chos kyi dbyings rgya mtsho thams cad rgyas par 'gegs
26b8(phal chen): pa mthong nas/dga' ba chen po'i shugs rgya mtsho shin tu yangs pa rab tu 'thob po//rigs kyi bu gzhan yang btsom ldan 'das rnam par snad mdzad kyi sku'i ba spu'i sgo re re nas sems kyi skad cig re re la kha dog
27a1(phal chen): @#//dri zhim po dang /'od gzer sna tshogs can gyi sprin sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad kyi rdul shin tu phra ba snyed 'byung zhing thams cad rgyas par 'gengs pa mthong nas/dga' ba chen po'i shugs rgya mtsho shin tu 
27a2(phal chen): yangs pa rab tu 'thob po//rigs kyi bu gzhan yang bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyi skul rnam par bltas na/sems kyi skad cig re re la mtshan re re nas/de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku gzugs mtshan 
27a3(phal chen): gyis rab tu brgyan pa'i sprin sangs rgyas kyi zhing mthas cad thams cad kyi rdul shinatu phra ba snyed byung ste/'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho thams cad rgyas par 'gegs pa mthong nas dga' ba chen po'i shugs rgyu mtsho shin tu yangs 
27a4(phal chen): pa rab tu 'thob po//rig kyi bu gzhan yang bcom dan 'das rnam par snang mdzod kyi sku'i dpe byad bzang po re re las sems kyi skad cig re re de bzhin gshegs pa sprul pa'i sku dpe byad bzang po brgyad cus shin tu 'bar 
27a5(phal chen): ba'i sprin sangs rgyas kyi zhi thams cad kyi rdul shin tu phra ba snyed byung ste/sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad rgyas par 'gengs shing de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi chos kyi 'khor lo'i dbyangs su sgrog pa'i 'brug sgra 'byin 
27a6(phal chen): pa mthong nas dga' ba chenapo'i shugs rgya mtsho shin tu yangs pa rab tu 'thob po//rigs kyi bu gzhan yang bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyi skul rnam par bltas na/sems kyi skad cig re re la ba spu'i sgo 
27a7(phal chen): thams cad nas sangs rgyas rnam par 'phrul pa'i sku dang po byang chub tu thugs bskyed pa dang bcas/pha rol tu phyin pa'i lam rnam par dag pa'i rgyan dang bcas/byang chub sems dpa'i sa yongs su gnon pa'i rnam par 
27a8(phal chen): 'phrul pa dang bcas pa sangs rgyas kyi zhing brjod du med pa'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed 'byung ba mthong nas dga' ba chen po'i shugs rgya mtsho shin du yangs pa rab tu 'thog po//rigs kyi bu gzhan la bcom ldan 'das 
27b1(phal chen): rnam par snang mdzad kyi sku las sems kyi skad cig re re la lha'i dbang po'i lus kyi sprin lha'i dbang po'i rnam par 'phrul pa dang bcas pa/sangs rgyas kyi zhing brjod du med pa'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed byung zhing 'jig rten gyi khams 
27b2(phal chen): thams cad rgyas par bkang ste/lha'i dbang po'i lus kyi spr-in yongs su bstan pas 'dul ba'i sems can rnams la mngon du gnas shing chos ston pa mthong nas/dga' ba chen po'i shugs rgya mtsho shin tu yangs pa rab tu 
27b3(phal chen): 'thob po//rigs kyi bu gzhan yang bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyi sku zhes sems kyi skad cig re res la klu'i dbang po'i lus kyi spyan la klu'i dbang po'i rnam par 'khrul pa dang bcas pa/sangasargyas kyi zhing brjod du 
27b4(phal chen): med pa'i rdul shin tu phrab snyed byung zhing 'jig rtenagyi khams thams cad rgyas par bkad ste/klu'i dbang po'i lus bstan pas 'dul ba'i semams can rnams la mngon gnas shing chos ston pa 'thob nas dga' ba chen po'i 
27b5(phal chen): shugs rgya mtsho shin tu yang pa rab tu 'thob po//rigs kyi bu ga gzhan yang bcom ldan 'da'as rnam par snang mdzad kyi/sku la rnam par bltas na/sems kyi skad cig re re la ba spu'i sgo thams cad nas gnod sbyin gyi dbang po'i 
27b6(phal chen): lus kyi sprin gyi gnod sbyin gyi dbang po'irnam par 'phrul pa dang bcas pa/sang rgyas kyi zhing brjod du med pa'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed byung zhing chos kyi dbyings thams cad rgyas par bskang ste/gnod sbyin gyi dbang po'ilus yongs 
27b7(phal chen): su bstan pas 'dul ba'i sems can rnams la mngon du gnas shing chos ston pa mthong nas/dga' ba chen po'i shugs rgya mtsho shin tu ya ngas pa rab tu 'thob po/rigs kyi bu gzhan yang bcom ldan 'angs rnam par snang 
27b8(phal chen): mdzad kyi sku la rnam par bltas na sems kyi skad cig re re las pa spu'i sgo thams cad nas/dri za'i dbang po'i lus kyi sprin dri za'i dbang po'i rnam par 'phrul pa dang bca' pa/sangs rgyas kyi zhing brjod du me dpa'i rdul shin tu 
28a1(phal chen): @#//phra ba snyed byung zhing chos kyi dbyings thams cad rgyas par bkang ste/dri za'i dbang po'i lus yongs su bstan pas 'dul ba'i sems can rnams la mngon du gnas shing chos ston pa mthong nas dga' ba chen po'i 
28a2(phal chen): shugs rgya mtsho shin tu yangs pa rab tu 'thob po//rigs kyi bu gzhan yang bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyi sku la rnam par bltas na sems kyi skad cig re re la pa spu'i sgo nas lha ma yin gyi dbang po'i lus kyi sprin lha ma 
28a3(phal chen): yin gyi dbang po'i rnam par 'phrul ba dang bcas pa/sangs rgyas kyi zhing brjod du med pa'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed byung zhing 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad rgyas par bkad ste/lha ma yin gyi dbang po'i las yongs su bstan 
28a4(phal chen): pas 'dul ba'i sems can de rnams la mngon par gnas shing /chos ston pa mthong nas dag pa chen po'i shugs rgya mtsho shin tu ya ngas pa rab tu 'thob bo//rigs kyi bu gzhan yangabacom ldan 'das rnam par dang mdzad 
28a5(phal chen): kyi skul rnam par bltas na/sems kyi skad cig re re ba spu'i sgo re re nas nam mkha'i lding gi dbang po'i lus kyi sprinanam mkha'i lding gi dbangapo'i rnam par 'phrul ba dad bcas pa/sangs rgyas kyis zhing brjod du med pa'i
28a6(phal chen): rdul shin tu phra ba snyed byang zhing 'jig rten gyi kham thams cad rgyas par bkang ste/nam mkha' lding gi dbang po'i lusayongs su bstan pas 'dul ba'i sems canarnams la mngon du gnas shing chos ston pa mthong nas/
28a7(phal chen): dga' ba chen po'i shugs rgya mtshoshin tu yangs pa rab tu 'thob po//gzhan yang rigs kyi bu bcom ldan 'das rnam parasnang mdzad kyi sku la rnam par bltas na sems kyi skad cig re rel ba spu'i sgo re re nas mi 'am ci'i
28a8(phal chen): dbang po'i lus kyi sprin mi 'am ci'i dbang po'i rnam par 'phrul ba dang bcas pa/sangs rgyas kyi zhing brjod du med pa'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed byung zhing chos kyi dbyings thams cad rgyas par bkad ste/mi 'am ci'i dbang po'i lus 
28b1(phal chen): yongs su bstan pas 'dul ba'i sems can rnams la mngon du gnas shing chos ston pa mthong nas/dga' ba chen po'i shugs rgya mtsho shin tu yangs pa rab tu 'thob po//rigs kyi bu gzhan yang bcom ldan 'das 
28b2(phal chen): rnam par snang mdzad kyi skul rnam par bltas na sems kyi skad cig re re la ba spu'i sgo re re la nas lto 'phye chen po'i lus kyi sprin lto 'phye chen po'i rnam par 'phrul ba dang bcas pa/sangs rgyas kyi zhing brjod du med pa'i rdul shin tu 
28b3(phal chen): phra ba snyed byung zhing chos kyi dbyings thams cad rgyas par bkang ste/lto 'phye chen po'i dbang po'i lus yongs su bstan pas 'dul pa'i sems can rnams la mdon du gnas shing chos ston mthong nas dga' ba chen po'i shugs 
28b4(phal chen): rgya mtsho shin tu yangs ba rab tu 'thob bo//rigs kyi bu gzhan yangabacom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyi sku la rnam par bltas na/sems kyi skad cig re re ba ba spu'i sgo re re mi nas mi'i dbang po'i lus kyi sprin mi'i dbang po'i 
28b5(phal chen): rnam par 'phrul ba dang bcad pa/sangs rgyas kyi zhing rjod du med pa'i rdul shinatu phra ba snyed byung zhing chos dbyings thams cad rgyas bar bkang ste/mi'i dbang po'i lus yongs bstan pas 'dul ba'i sems can rnams

28b8(phal chen): thams cad rgyas par bkang ste/tshangs pa'i dbang po'i lusayongs su bstan pas 'dul ba'i sesam can rnams la mngon du gnas shing tshangs pa'i sgra skad dang dbyangs kyis chos ston pa mthong nas sems kyi 
29a1(phal chen): @#//skad cig re re la thams cad mkhyen pa nyid dang ldan pa'i dga' ba chen po'i shugs rgya mtsho chos kyi dbyings ltar rgya che zhing shin tu yangs pa rab tu 'thob ste/sngon thob par gyur pa 'thob par 'gyur ba ma yin 
29a2(phal chen): no//sngon khong du chud par 'gyur ba ma yin no//sngon zhugs pa la 'jug par 'gyur ba ma yin no//sngon khyab par gyur par khyab par 'gyur pa ma yin no//sngon mthong ba mthong bar 'gyur ba ma yin no/

29a7(phal chen): rgyas par 'gengs pas gting med pa'o//rnam par tharab pa 'di ni dmigs pa gcig tu rnam par 'phrul pa thams cad yongs su 'du bar byed pas sgo thams cad dang ldan pa'o//rnam par thar pa'i 'di ni chos thams cad lus dang 
29a8(phal chen): mi gnyis pas spyod pas don yod pa'o//rnam par thar pa'adi ni sgyu ma lta bur yongs su spyod pas ma skyes pa'o//rnam par thar pa ni thams cad mkhyen pa nid kyi smon lam gyi gzugs brnyan du byung bas gzugs

33b4(phal chen): byed do//don dang tshig dang yi ge 'dzin pa'i shugs kyis rnam par gnon pa'i gzugs kyi mthus yongs su 'dzin to//chos kyi dkyil 'khor thams cad rnam par dag pa'i snying po khong du chud pa'i shes rab kyis yongs su spyod
