91a6(phal chen): rgya mtsho mang por bsgom par byas//tshul med mtha' dang dbus med mnyam med pas//skad cig re re dag tu rnam par spel//thub pa'i bdag po ji snyed mthong gyur pa//de dag spyan sngar sngan chad 'di thob 
91a7(phal chen): ste//sgo tshul rnam pa gzhan dang gzhan dag gis//de dag gis ni rnam thar 'di yang bstan//de dag rnam thar ma nor bsam yas la//bskal pa bye ba mang por kho mos bslabs//de la rgyal bas bkod 
91a8(phal chen): cing grol gyur ba//de dag dam chos sprin las cig car 'thung //phyogs bcu kun gyi zhing rnams thams cad du//thogs pa med par lus gyis rgyas par 'gengs//skad cig re rer zhing rgyud bsam yas 
91b1(phal chen): pa'i||dus gsum rgyud rnams kun la'ang rab tu 'jug/dus gsum rgyal ba rgya mtsho ma lus pa//re re kun la'ang mngon du rab tu lta//de dag lus kyi gzugs bsnyan rgyal ba la//sprin tu gyur pa rnams 
91b2(phal chen): kyang spyan snga 'dri ma//phyogs rnams kun tu nye bar phyin 'dod zhing //rgyal ba'i spyan sngar 'dod pas shin tu mdzes//rgyan rnams ma lus mngon par char 'bebs shing //rgyal ba rnams la de dag mchod 
91b3(phal chen): pa byed//sangs rgyas rgya mtsho che la rnam yangs pa'i zhu pa mtha' yas kun kyang zhu pa byed//rgyal ba'i chos sprin mngon par char 'bab pa//kun kyang ma lus yongs su 'dzin pa byed//phyogs bcu ma lus 
91b4(phal chen): kun tu de dag 'dod//rgyal ba'i dkyil 'khor kun gyi spyan sngar 'beb//gzugs mang rnam pa sna tshogs stan gnas shing //rnam 'phrul sna tshogs nye bar ston pa byed//kha dog mtha' yas rang gi lus 
91b5(phal chen): gzugs gyis//phyogs rnams thams cad rnam pa stong du 'gengs//de dag lus gcig las kyang gzugs kyi rnams//mtha' dang dbus med rnam par ston pa byed//ba spu re re'i sgo nas grangs med pa'i//
91b6(phal chen): 'od gzer mang po rgya mtsho de dag 'gyed//skye dgu'i nyon mongs me'i gdud pa dag/sna tshogs thabs gyis rab tu zhi bar byed//'di la gnas pa lus sprul sprin mang pa/ba spu re re'i sgo nas 'gyed byed cing ngo mtshar 
91b7(phal chen): rmad kyis phyogs kun rgyas bkang nas//chos chu'i char gyis sems can rnam par 'dul//tshul 'jug 'di ni bsam gyis mi khyab gzugs//rgyal sras kun gyis gnas su gyur pa ste//de la gnas shing 
91b8(phal chen): phyi mtha'i bskal par yang //zhing rnams kun tu spyad pa rab tu spyod//bsam pa ji bzhin chos rnams ston byed cing //de dag lta ba'i dra ba rnam par bzlog/mtho ris mya ngan 'das la sems can 'god//
92a1(phal chen): @#//thams cad mkhyen pa'i sa yang rnam par ston//'jig rten skye gnas thams cad ma lus par//kha dog mtha' yas lung med bsam yas pa//bsam pa bzhin du chos rnams rjod byed de//
92a2(phal chen): de dag sems can kun gyi gzugs brnyan gzugs//'di dang gzhan dag tshad las 'das pa'i//yon tan rgya mtsho'i de med rgya mtsho'i//rdul phran rab snyed bsam gyis mi khyab pa//rnam thar zhi ba 'di 
92a3(phal chen): lan thob par 'gyur//rigs kyi bu lo mos byang chub sems dpa'i rnam par thar pa/sems can thams cad yongs su smin par bya bas bsam pa ji lta ba bzhin du dge ba'i rtsa ba la yang dag par skul ba 
92a4(phal chen): 'di shes par zad na/kho mos byang chub sems dpa' 'jig rten thams cad gyi lam gyi rgyud las 'das pa/'jig rten gyi skye gnas thams cad du gzugs brnyan rab tu 'byung ba/'jig rten gyi shes pa 
92a5(phal chen): thams cad las yang dag par 'das pa/shes bya'i sgrib pa thams cad kyi ri rnam par 'thor ba la brtson pa/chos thams cad kyi rang bzhin dang /mtshan nyid rab tu rtogs pa nyon mongs pa'i sgrib pa dang /
92a6(phal chen): mun pa thams cad rnam par sel ba la brtson pa/chos tham cad rab tu dpyad pas mngon par bsgrub pa la mkhas pa/bdag med pa'i chos mngon du gyur pa/sems can thams can yongs su smin par bya 
92a7(phal chen): ba dang /gdul ba rgyun mi gcod pa/chos kyi dbyings kyi tshul gyi gnyis su med par rab tu rtogs pa/dag gi lam gyi tshul rgya mtsho thams cad kyi rjes su 'brang ba'i blo dang ldan pa/de dag gi spyod pa 
92a8(phal chen): shes pa 'am/yon tan rgya mtsho la 'jug pa 'am/ye shes kyis rnam par gnon pa yongs su shes par bya ba 'am/sems kyi gnas khong du chud par bya ba 'am/ting nge 'dzin la dbang sgyur ba rtogs 
92b1(phal chen): par bya ba 'am/rnam par thar pas rnam par 'phrul pa yongs su shes par bya bar nus re skan//song rigs kyi bu 'dzam bu'i gling 'di nyid kyi lum bi ni'i nags tshal na/lum bi'i na'i nags tshal gyi lha mo gzi 
92b2(phal chen): brjid kyi dkyil 'khor bzang pos dga' ba'i dpal zhes bya ba 'dug gis/de'i gan du song la ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs su byung ba yin pa dang /ji ltar na 'jig rten rnams 
92b3(phal chen): la snang ba byed par 'gyur pa dang /ji ltar na phyi ma'i mtha'i bskal pa'i mur thugs par byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa spyad pas mi skyo bar 'gyur pa dris shig/de nas tshong dpon gyi bu nor bzangs kyis mtshan mo'i lha 
92b4(phal chen): mo 'gro ba thams cad bsrung ba'i smon lam la brtson pa'i 'od kyi rkang pa gnyis la spyi bos phyag 'tshal te/mtshan ma'i lha mo 'gro ba thams cad bsrug pa'i smon lam la brtson pa'i 'do la lan brgya stong du mar g.yas 
92b5(phal chen): phyogs su bskor ba byas nas/thal mo sbyar te phyir lta zhing mtshan mo'i lha mo 'gro ba thams can bsrung ba'i smon lam la rtson pa'i 'od kyi drung nas song ngo ////
92b6(phal chen): @#//sangs rgyas rmad gcad ces bya ba shin tu rgyas pa chen po'i mdo//bam po bdun pa//de nas tshong dpon gyi pu nor bzangs mtshan mo'i lha mo 'gro ba 
92b7(phal chen): thams cad bsrung ba'i smonalam la brtson pa'i 'od kyis gdams ngag de rjes su dran par byed cing /byang chub sems dpa'i rnam par thar pa sems can thams cad yongs su smin par bya bas bsam pa ji lta ba bzhin du dge ba'i rtsa ba la 
92b8(phal chen): yang dag par skul ba de la yongs su bsgom pa dang /khong du chud par bya ba dang /rgya cher byed cing mthar gyis lum bi ni'i nags tshal gang na ba der phyin te/lum bi'i ni'i nags tshal la g.yas phyogs su bskor 
93a1(phal chen): @#//ba byas nas/lum bi ni'i nags tshal na rin po che'i shing gzi brjid kyi dkyil 'khor bzang pos dga' ba'i dpal yongs su tshol tshol ba las/lum bi'i nags tshal gyi lha mo gzi brjid kyi dkyil
93a2(phal chen): 'khor bzang pos dga' ba'idapal lum bi ni'i nags tshal ni rin po che'i shing gi yal ga thams cad kyi dkyil 'khor gyi khyim brtsegs pa zhig na/seng ge'i khri rin po che'i pad mo'i snying po can zhig la 'dug cing /
93a3(phal chen): nags tshal gyi lha mo khrag khrig brgya stong phrag nyi shu'i 'khor gyis kun nas bskor te/mdun du byas nas chos ston to//byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyi skye ba rgya mtshos shin tu bstan pa zhes bya ba'i mdo
93a4(phal chen): sde yongs su ston cing de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs kyi rgyud du rigs dang ldan par skyes pa'i byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi yon tan rgya mtsho'i shugs rnam par 'phel bar byed cing 'dug pa mthong nas
93a5(phal chen): lum bi ni'i nags tshal gyi lha mo gzibarjid kyi dkyil 'khor bzang pos dga' ba'i dpal gang na ba der song ste/phyin nas lum bi ni'i nags tshal gyilha mo gzi brjid kyi dkyil 'khor bzad pos dga' 
93a6(phal chen): ba'i dpal gyi rkang pa gnyis la spyi bos phyag 'tshal nas/mdun du 'dug ste thal mo spyar nas 'di skad ces smras so//'phags ma bdag gis bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sems
93a7(phal chen): bskyed na/ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' rnam de bzhin gshegs pa'i rig su byung ba lags pa'ang ma 'tshal/ji ltar na byang chub sem dpa' rnams spyod pa la spyod cing sems can rnams la
93a8(phal chen): snang ba 'byin par 'gyur ba yang ma 'tshal to//de skad ces smras pa dang /lum bi ni'i nags tshal gyis lha mo gzi brjid kyi dkyi' 'khor bzang po dga' ba'i dpal gyis tshong dpon gyi bu nor bzangs
93b1(phal chen): la 'di skad ces smras so//rigs kyi bu bcu po 'di dag na byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi skye ba ste/de dag dang ldan na byang chub sems dpa'-i rnams de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs su skyes pa yin na//
93b2(phal chen): de dag la byung na byang chub sems dpa' rarnams sems kyi skad cig re re la'ang byang chub sems dpa'i dge ba'i rtsa bas rnam par 'phel bar 'gyur te thogs pa med pa dang /zhum pa med pa dang /
93b3(phal chen): phyir ldog pa med pa dang /rgyun gcod pa med pa dang /yongs su skyo ba med pa dang /sgyid lug par 'gyur ba med pa dang /nyams par 'gyur ba med pa dang/nyam dma' bar 'gyur ba med pa dang /
93b4(phal chen): yongs su yi 'chad par 'gyur ba med pa dang/chung za bar 'gyur ba med pa dang/thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi rjes su 'brang bar 'gyur ba dang /chos kyi dbyings kyi tshul la yongs su gzhol bar 'gyur
93b5(phal chen): ba dang /sangs rgyas kyi byang chub tu yongs su smin pa 'thob par 'gyur ba dang /byang chub sems dpa'i sems bskyed pa shin tu yangs par 'gyur ba dang /pha rol tu phyin pa thams cad kyis rnam par 'phel bar
93b6(phal chen): 'gyur ba dang /'jig rten gyi lam gyi rgyud thams cad las rnam par ldog par 'gyur ba dang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i sar yongs su 'grub par 'gyur ba dang /ye shes kyis mngon par shes pa shin tu byang
93b7(phal chen): bar 'gyur ba dang/sangs rgyas kyi chos mngon du 'gyur ba dang /thams cad mkhyen pa'i yul gyi don dang ldan par 'gyur ro//bcu gang zhe na 'di lta ste sangs rgyas thams cad la bsnyen bkur ba'i smon lam gyis
93b8(phal chen): sbyor ba'i snying po zhes bya ba ni byang chub sems dpa'i skye ba dang po'o//byang chub kyi sems kyi yan lag yongs su grub par 'gyur ba 'byung ba'i snying po zhes bya ba na byang chub sems dpa'i skye gnyis pa'o//
94a1(phal chen): @#//chos kyi tshul la nges par rtogs par gzhag pa'i sbyor ba'i tshul mngon du gyur pa 'byung ba'i snying po zhes bya ba ni byang chub sems dpa'i skye ba gsum pa'o//dus gsum snang bar bya ba'i lhag pa'i bsam pa 
94a2(phal chen): rnam par dag pa'i snying po zhes bya ba ni byad chub sems dpa'i skye ba bzhi pa'o//kun nas snang ba'i 'od kyi snying po zhes bya ba ni byan chub sems dpa'i skye ba lnga pa'o//de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs kyi rgyud du 'byung 
94a3(phal chen): ba'i snying po zhes bya ba ni bang chub sems dpa'i skye badrug pa'o//de bzhin gshegs ba thams cad kyi stobs kyi 'od snang ba'i rgyan gyi snying po zhes bya ba ni byams chub sems dpa'i skye bab bdun pa'o//ye shes kyi sgo kun nas 
94a4(phal chen): rnam par dpyad pas yongs su 'grub pa 'byung ba'i snying po zhe bya ba ni byang chub sems pa'i skye ba brgyad pa'o//chos kyi dbyings sprul pas brgyan pa byed pa'i snying po zhes bya ba na byang chub sems dpa'i skyed ba dgu pa'o//
94a5(phal chen): de bzhin gshegs pa'i sa yongs su gnon pa'i shugs drag po'i snying po zhes bya ba ni dbyang chub sems dpa'i skye ba ni byang chub sems dpa'i skye pa dang po sangs rgyas thams cad la bsnyen 
94a6(phal chen): bkur ba'i smon lam gyis sbyor ba'i snying po zhes bya ba gang zhe na/rigs kyi bu 'di la byang chub sems dpa' thog mar sangs rgyas mchod cing rim gror bya ba la 'jug ste/de sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams la 
94a7(phal chen): rim gro dang /bkur sti dang /sti stang dang /mchod pa byed cing bsnyen bkur byed de/dgyes par byed la thugs byung bar mi byed pas/de bzhin gshegs pa'i zhal blta bas mi ngoms pa sangs rgyas la rim 
94a8(phal chen): gror bya bar brtson pas/sangs rgyas la dga' ba'i shugs kyis rnam par spel ba'i shugs dang ldan pa/de bzhin gshegs pa mthong bas mos pa'i shugs drag po yongs su skyes pa/de phyir spyi ldog 
94b1(phal chen): pa'i dang pas bsod nams yongs su sdud pa'i tshe/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad la mchod pa'i tshogs yongs su bsdu ba la brtson zhing sbyor ba rgyun mi gcod pa yin te//rigs kyi bu de ni byang chub sems 
94b2(phal chen): dpa'i rnams kyi skye ba dang po sangs rgyas thams cad la bsnyen bkur ba'i smon lam gyis sbyor ba'i snying po zhes bya ba ste/thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi tshogs dge ba'i rtsa ba yongs su 'grub pa yang dag par sdud par 'gyur 
94b3(phal chen): ba'o//rigs kyi bu de la byang chub sems dpa'i skye ba gnyis pa/byang chub kyi sems kyi yan lag yongs su grub par 'gyur ba 'byung ba'i snying po zhes bya ba gang zhe na/rigs kyi bu 'di la byang chub sems dpa' bla 
94b4(phal chen): na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sems bskyed de/'di ltar sems can thams cad yongs su bskyab pa'i phyir snying rje chen po'i sems dang /shin tu dgyes par bya ba'i phyir sangs rgyas thams cad 
94b5(phal chen): bsnyen bkur ba'i sems dang /dngos po thams cad la 'phangs par mi brtsi ba'i phyir/sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad yongs su brtsal ba'i sems dang /thams cad mkhyen pa nyidamangon du 'gyur bar bya ba'i 
94b6(phal chen): phyir/brtsam pa chen po'i sems dang /'gro ba thams cad yongs su bsdu ba'i sbyor bas rgyas par dgang ba'i phyir byams pa chen po'i sems dang thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi go ca brtan par bya ba'i phyir 'gro 
94b7(phal chen): ba thams cad yongs su mi gtong ba'i sems dang /yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhin du shes pa'i snang ba rab tu thob par bya ba'i phyir g.yo dang sgyu med pa'i sems dang /byang chub sems dpa'i lam la nan tan tu bya ba'i 
94b8(phal chen): kha dog tu lus 'khrus pa'i sems dang /de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi smon lam dang mi 'gal bar bya ba'i phyir/sangs rgyas thams cad la sku bslu pa med pa'i sems dang /phyi ma'i 
95a1(phal chen): @#//mtha'i mur thug par sems can thams cad yongs su smin par bya ba dang /gdul ba rgyun mi gcad pa'i phyir thams cad mkhyen pa nyid du smon lam chen po 'bebs pa'i sems dang /de dag las 
95a2(phal chen): stsogs pa byang chub kyi sems kyi yan lag gi tshogs sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi rdul shin tu phra ba snyed kyis yongs su rdzogs pa'i byang chub sems dpa' ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs su skyes pa yin te/rigs kyi 
95a3(phal chen): bu de ni byang chub sems dpa'i skye ba gnyis pa/byang chub kyi sems kyi yan dag yongs su 'grub par 'gyur ba 'byung ba'i snying po zhes bya ba'o//rigs kyi bu de la byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi byang chub 
95a4(phal chen): sems dpa'i skye ba gsum pa chos kyi tshul la nges par gzhag pa'i sbyor ba'i tshul mngon du gyur pa 'byung ba'i snying po zhes pa bya ba gang zhe na/rigs kyi bu 'di la byang chub sems dpa' chos thams cad kyi tshul rgya mtsho yod de 
95a5(phal chen): chog nges par rtogs pas mngon du bya ba'i sems dang ldan pa/thams cad mkhyen pa'i lam gyi rnam pa yongs su rdzogs par bya bas mngon du bya bar gcig tu sems dpa'i sems dang ldan pa/kha na ma tho ba med 
95a6(phal chen): pa'i las la yang dag par spyad par sems pa'i sems dang ldan pa/byang chub sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin gyi tshul rgya mtsho thams cad yongs su dag par bya ba'i sems dang ldan ba byang chub sems dpa'i yon tan 
95a7(phal chen): thams cad la nan tan du bya bas yongs su grub par bya ba'i sems dang lda pa/byang chub sems dpa' lam gyi rgyan thams cad mngon par bsgrub pa'i sems dang ldan pa/tham sa cad mkhyen pa nyid du dmigs 
95a8(phal chen): pa'i tshogs rgya mtsho chen po yongs su bsgrub pa bskal pa'i me 'bar bas kyang /rgyun mi gcad par brtson par bya ba'i sems dang ltan pa/'gro ba thams cad yongs su smin par bya ba dang /gdul ba la 
95b1(phal chen): rab tu zhugs pas byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa kun tu bzang po mngon par sgrub pa'i sems dang ldan pa/yongs su spyad pa'i tshul thams cad la rab tu zhugs pa ni byang chub sems dpa'i yon tan yongs su rdzogs par
95b2(phal chen): bya ba la dngos po thams cad rnam par bsgom pas dngos po med pa'i tshul la zhugs pa'i sems dang ldan pa yin te/rigs kyi bu 'di ni byang chub sems dpa'i skye ba gsum pa chos kyi tshul la nges par rtogs pas gzhag
95b3(phal chen): ba'i sbyor ba'i tshul mngon tu gyur pa la 'byung ba'i snying po zhes bya ba'o//rigs kyi bu de la byang chub sems dpa'i skye pa bzhi pa dus gsum snang bar bya ba'i lhag pa'i bsam pa rnam par dag pa'i snying po zhes bya ba gang zhe na/
95b4(phal chen): rigs kyi bu 'di la byang chub sems dpa'i lhag pa'i bsam pa'i dbyings la gya nom par yongs su dag pa yin/sangs rgyas kyi byang chub la snang ba rab tu thog pa yin byang chub sems dpa'i tshul rgya mtsho la rab tu zhugs pa
95b5(phal chen): yin/bsam pa brtan pa rdo rje'i khams lta bus yongs su zin pa'i sems dang ldan pas shin tu brtan pa yin/srid pa'i lam gyi rgyud du skye pa las phyin phyogs su phyogs pa yin/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi
95b6(phal chen): rnam par 'phrul pa yongs su grub par bya ba la mngon du gyur pa yin/byang chub sems dpa'i dbang po rnam par spel ba la khyad par du 'bogs pa rab tu thob pa yin/lhag pa'i bsam pa shin tu 'ong gsal bar bya ba'i
95b7(phal chen): phyir sems shin tu dge ba yin/smon lam brtan zhing rgya che ba rnam par 'phel bar bya ba la mi g.yo ba yin/sgrib pa thams cad kyi ri rnam par gtor ba'i phyir de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad gyis yang dag par
95b8(phal chen): dgongs pa yin/'gro ba thams cad nye bar 'tsho bas mgon du gyur pa yin te//rigs kyi bu 'di ni byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi byang chub sems dpa'i skye ba bzhi pa dus gsum snang bar bya ba'i lhag pa'i bsam
96a1(phal chen): @#//pa rnam par dag pa'i snying po zhes bya ba'o//rigs kyi bu de la byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi byang chub sems dpa'i skye ba lnga pa kun nas snang ba'i 'od kyi snying po zhes bya ba gang zhe na/rigs kyi bu 'di la byang chub 
96a2(phal chen): sems dpa' 'gro ba thams cad yongs su smin par bya ba dang /gdul ba la rab tu zhugs pas sbyor ba phun sum tshogs pa yin/sbyin pa lhug par gtong bas dngos po'i 'du shes thams cad las gyen du 
96a3(phal chen): 'phags pa yin/tshul khrims mtha' yas pas mtha' las rgal par rnam par dag pa yin/sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad la bzod pa'i snang ba rab tu thob pa yin/de bzhin gshegs pa'i yul la 
96a4(phal chen): gnas pa'i bzod pa dang ldan pa yin thams cad mkhyen pa nyid du yangs par 'byung ba la zhugs pas brtson 'grus chen po dang ldan pa yin/sgo kun tu lta ba'i ting nge 'dzin gyi ye shes kyi dkyil 'khor rnam par 
96a5(phal chen): dag pas bsam gtan la rab tu brtson pa yin/chos snang ba thams cad rab tu thob pas shes rab kyi mthu'i 'od dang ldan pa yin/sangs rgyas blta ba rgya mtsho rnam par dmigs par bya ba la zhugs pas 
96a6(phal chen): thogs pa med pa'i mig dang ldan pa yin/'jig rten thams cad yongs su tshim par byed pas/chos thams cad kyi de kho na nyid la dbang sgyur bas smos pa yin/chos kyi tshul yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhin 
96a7(phal chen): du thob par bya ba la rab tu brtson pa yin te/rigs kyi bu 'di ni byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi byang chub sems dpa'i skye ba lnga pa kun nas snang ba'i 'od kyi snying po zhes bya ba'o//rigs kyi bu de la byang 
96a8(phal chen): chub sems dpa' rnams kyis byang chub sems dpa'i skye bo drug pa de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs kyi rgyad du byung ba'i snying po zhes bya ba gang zhe na/rigs kyi bu 'di la byang chub sems dpa' de bzhin 
96b1(phal chen): gshegs pa'i rigs su byung ba yin/de bzhin gshegs pa'i rgyud du skyes pa yin/tshul gyi sgo thams cad la shin tu grub pa yin/'das pa dang /ma byon pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i de bzhin 
96b2(phal chen): gshegs pa rnams kyi smon lam chen po la rnam par dag pa yin/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi dge ba'i rtsa ba la rang bzhin gcig pa'i dge ba'i rtsa bar gyur pa yin/sangs rgyas thams cad dang lus 
96b3(phal chen): gcig tu gyur pa yin/chos dkar pos 'jig rten gzhan du 'gro ba yin/sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs yongs su bstan pa'i ting nge 'dzin la bdag nyid che ba'i chos kyis rnam par spyod pa yin/dus la 
96b4(phal chen): ji ltar 'bab pa bzhin du sems can rnam par dag par bya ba'i chos la zhugs pa yin/sangs rgyas kyi chos yongs su dris pa la spobs pa'i rgyun mi 'chad pa yin te/rigs kyi bu 'di ni byang chub sems 
96b5(phal chen): dpa' rnams kyi byang chub sems dpa'i skye ba drug pa de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs kyi rgyud du 'byung ba'i snying po zhes bya ba'o//rigs kyi bu de la byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi byang chub 
96b6(phal chen): sems dpa'i skye ba bdun pa de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi stobs kyi 'od snang ba'i rgyan gyi snying po zhes bya ba gang zhe na/rigs kyi bu 'di la byang chub sems dpa' sangs rgyas kyi stobs la 'jug pa'i snang bas sangs rgyas kyi zhing du 'gro ba las 
96b7(phal chen): phyir mi ldog go/byang chub sems dpa'i yon tan rgya mtsho las phyir ldog par 'gyur ro//chos thams cad sgyu ma'i rnam pa ltar yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhin du shes par bya ba la gnas pas yongs su mi 'chad par mi 
96b8(phal chen): 'gyur ro//'jig rten thams cad rmi lam lta bur rab tu rtogs par byed do//gzugs su rnam par dmigs pa'i gnas thams cad gzugs brnyan lta bur mngon par bsgrub po//mngon par shes pas 
97a1(phal chen): @#//rnam par 'phrul pa sprul pa lta bu la dbang rab tu thob par byed do//srid pa thams cad du skye ba grib ma lta bur gdong rab tu ston to//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi chos kyi 'khor lo sgra brnyan 
97a2(phal chen): lta bu rab tu shes so//thabs kyi tshul don sna tshogs dang ldan pa la rab tu brtson pas chos kyi dbyings kyi tshul rab tu bstan pa la pha rol tu phyin pa'i dam pa thob pa yin te/rigs kyi bu 'di ni byang chub sems 
97a3(phal chen): dpa' rnams kyi byang chub sems dpa'i skye ba bdun pa de gshegs pa thams cad kyi stobs kyi 'od snang ba'i rgyan gyi snying po zhes bya ba'o//rigs kyi bu de la byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi byang chub sesams dpa'i skye ba 
97a4(phal chen): brgyad pa ye shes kyi sgo kun nas rnam par dpyad pas yongs su 'grub pa 'byung ba'i snying po zhes bya ba gang zhe na/rigs kyi bu 'di la byang chub sems dpa'agazho nur gyur cing byang chub sems dpa'i rnam par dgod 
97a5(phal chen): pa la rnam par gnas pa yin te/de la gnas nas thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes kyi tshul la rnam par dpyod de/ye shes kyi tshul gyi sgo re re la yang byang chub sems dpa'i spyod yul tshad med par yongs su ston 
97a6(phal chen): pas bskal pa tshad med par zad par 'gyur ro//byang chub sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin thams cad la'ang pha rol tu phyin pa'i dam bcas pas dbang du 'gyur ro//sems kyi skad cig re re zhing sems kyi skad 
97a7(phal chen): cig thams cad la yang phyogs bcu'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing brjod du med pa dag na bzhugs pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi zhabs kyi drung du skyes te/dmigs pa tha mi dad pas tha mi dad pa'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms 
97a8(phal chen): par 'jug go/tha mi dad pa'i chos rnams la tha dad pa ye shes kyi dbang yongs su ston to//dmigs pa brjod du med pa dag kyang sgrib pa med pa'i dbyings su non par byed do//dmigs pa chu dus kyang 
97b1(phal chen): mtha' yas par bstan pa'i sa la yongs su 'jug go/chos nyid tshad med pa yang chung ba 'am/che bar khong du chud par byed do//'jig rten thams cad kyang rnam par dmigs pa dang mnyam par rab 
97b2(phal chen): tu rtogs par byed do//chos kyi dmigs pa thams cad dang /rnam par shes pa'i rgyud thams cad kyang bsgom pas khong du chud par byed do//rigs kyi bu 'di ni byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi byang 
97b3(phal chen): chub sems dpa'i skye ba brgyad pa ye shes kyi sgo kun nas rnam par dpyad pas yongs su 'grub pa 'byung ba'i snying po zhes bya ba'o//rigs kyi bu de la byang chub sems dpa' rnam kyi byang chub sems dpa'i skye 
97b4(phal chen): ba dgu pa chos kyi dbyings sprul pas brgyan pa byed pa'i snying po zhes bya ba gang zhe na//rigs kyi bu 'di la byang chub sems dpa' sems kyi skad cig thams cad la rgyan sna tshogs dang /rgyan du mar ldan pa'i 
97b5(phal chen): sangs rgyas kyi zhing byin gyis rlob ste/sprul pa thams cad la mi 'jigs pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa'i dam pa thob pa yin/sangs rgyas kyi sprul pa thams cad la shin tu mkhas par gyur pa yin/chos kyi sprul 
97b6(phal chen): pa la mi 'jigs pa rnam par dag pa yin/chos kyi dbyings dam pa chags pa med pa'i spyod yul dang ldan pa yin/bsam pa ji lta ba bzhin du sems can gyi lus rnam par bya ba'i byin gyi rlabs la mkhas 
97b7(phal chen): pa yin//sems can gdul ba la mkhas pa bsam gyis mi khyab pa dang ldan pa yin/spyod pa sna tshogs kyis mngon par 'tshang rgya ba yongs su ston pa yin/thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi lam sgrib pa 
97b8(phal chen): med pa mngon par bsgrub pa la mkhas pa yin/de'i rgyud kyi chos kyi 'khor lo skor ba la mkhas pa yongs su ston pa yin/sems can mtha' dang dbus med pa gdul ba'i thabs mngon par bsgrub pa la 
98a1(phal chen): @#//mkhas pa yin/rnam par snang mdzad kyi ye shes kyi dbyig du bya ba'i mdzod dang ldan pas sems can gdul ba'i dus la bab na rtag tu sta gon du gnas pa yin te//rigs kyi bu 'di ni byang chub sems 
98a2(phal chen): dpa' rnams kyi byang chub sems dpa'i skye ba dgu pa chos kyi dbyings sprul pas brgyan pa byed pa'i snying po zhes bya ba'o//rigs kyi bu de la byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi byang chub kyi sems dpa'i skye ba bcu pa de bzhin 
98a3(phal chen): gshegs pa'i sa yongs su gnon pa'i shugs drag po zhes bya ba gang zhe na/'di la byang chub sems dpa' dus gsum gyi de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad dang /rang bzhin gcig pa'i yul la dbang skur 
98a4(phal chen): ba yin te/'jig rten gyi khams thams cad mdab chags pa'i yul la rab tu 'jug pa yin/sems can thams cad kyi sngon gyi mtha' dang phyin ma'i mtha'i shi 'pho dang /skye ba'i sems rgyud chags par 
98a5(phal chen): 'byung ba yang rab tu shes so//byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyi ye shes mdab chags pa'i yul yang rab tu shes so//'das pa ngang ma byon pa dang da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas thams cad kyi mngon par rdzogs 
98a6(phal chen): par 'tshang rgya ba mdab chags pa yang rab tu shes so/chos thams cad kyi rgyud mdab chags pa la mkhas par bya ba yang rab tu shes so//bskal pa 'jig pa dang 'du ba thams cad kyi sngon gyi mtha'i dang phyi mtha' dang /
98a7(phal chen): da ltar byung ba nams kyang ming dang bcas/sa tshigs dang bcas par mdab chags par rab tu shes so//sems can tshul ji lta bur yongs su smin par byas pa dang /dus la gang du 'bab pa rnams la mngon 
98a8(phal chen): par rdzogs par 'tshang rgya ba'i rgyan dang /rnam par 'tshang rgya bas gdul bar bya ba yongs su bstan pa'i byin gyi rlabs kyi ye shes kyang mngon par bsgrub po//sangs rgyas 'byung ba thams cad mngon par 'tshang rgya 
98b1(phal chen): ba'i drung du nye bar 'gro zhing chos kyi 'khor lo skor ba la mkhas pa'i tshul mdab chags pa yang/sems can gyi khams mtha' dang dbus med pa gdul ba'i thabs mngon par bsgrub pa la mkhas pas yongs 
98b2(phal chen): su ston te/rigs kyi bu 'di ni byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi byang chub sems dpa'i skye ba bcu pa de bzhin gshegs pa'i sa med pa'i chos nyid rab tu rtogs pa dang/mtshan ma med pa'i mtha'i sgo dang 
98b3(phal chen): ldan pa'i ye shes mtshan ma thams cad dang bral bas sa bon las myu gu chud mi za ba'i chos nyid dang/rgya las rgya'i gzugs brnyan 'byung ba'i chos nyid dang/gzugs las gzugs brnyan 'byung ba'i
98b4(phal chen): chos nyid dang/sgra brnyan dang sgrar mnyam par dmigs pa'i chos nyid dang rnam par dpyod pa dang/rmi lam du mnyam par rnam par dmigs pa'i chos nying dang/sgyu ma'i rnam pa ltar las las yongs su byung 
98b5(phal chen): ba'i chos nyid dang/sems gzugs med pas 'jig rten yongs su bsgrod pa'i chos nyid dang/rkyen dang rgyu ji ltar bsags pa bzhin du 'bras bu 'byung ba'i chos nyid dang /las ji ltar bsags pa dang 
98b6(phal chen): mnyam pa rnam par smin pa 'byung ba'i chos nyid dang/thabs mkhas pas rnam par bsgrubs pa'i chos nyid dang/chos dang chos ma yin pa mnyam pa nyid kyis mngon par brlan pa'i chos nyid dang/ye shes kyi
98b7(phal chen): rjes su 'jug pa de lta bus mngon par bsgrubs pa'i 'du shes dang/yid la bya bas tshong dpon gyi bu nor bzangs kyis khang pa brtsegs pa rnam par snang mdzad kyi rgyan gyis brgyan pa'i snying po'i sgo nas rnam pa de
98b8(phal chen): lta bur btung de phyag 'tshal nas yun ring mo zhig lon pa dang/dge ba'i rtsa ba'i shugs bsams gyis mi khyab pas mngon par brlan pa'i sems dang ldan pa/lus dang sems shin tu sim par gyur pa 
99a1(phal chen): @#//de khang pa brtsegs pa'i sgo'i gnas nas langs te /yud cam zhig du khang pa brtsegs pa chen po rnam par snang mdzad kyi rgyan gyis brgyan pa'i snying po de la myig mi 'gebs pas bltas nas thal mo
99a2(phal chen): sbyar te lan brgya stong du mar g.yas phyogs su bskor pa byas nas/de lta bu'i 'du shes dang yid la bya bas bskul pa'i sems dang ldan pas tshig du brjod pa/'di ni stong pa nyid dang /mtshan ma med pa dang /
99a3(phal chen): smon pa myed pa'i gnas la rnam par spyod pa rnams kyi gnas so//'di ni chos thams cad la rnam par mi rtog pa'i gnas la rnam par sbyod pa rnams kyi gnas so//chos kyi dbyings tha mi dad pa'i gnang
99a4(phal chen): la rnam par spyod pa sems can gyi khams mi dmigs pa'i gnas la rnam par spyad pa/chos thams cad mi skye pa'i gnas la rnam par dpyod pa 'jig rten thams cad gnas med pa'i gnas la rnam par s+yod pa/
99a5(phal chen): 'gro pa thams cad kyi gnas/gnas med pa'i gnas la rnal par spyod pa/gnas thams cad rnam par bsgoms pa'i gnas la rnam par par spyod pa/brten pa thams cad la mi rten pa'i gnas la rnam par
99a6(phal chen): spyod pa/lus thams cad la mi rten pa gnas la rnam par song ba/'du shes kyi rnam pa thams cad bstsal pa'i gnas la rnam par sbyod pa rnams kyi gnas so/'di ni chos thams cad dang rang bzhin
99a7(phal chen): med pa'i gnas la rnam pa ra spyod pa rnams kyi gnas so//rtog pa dang rnam par rtog pa dang /yongs su rtog pa thams cad du rnam par mi rtog pa'i gnas la rnam par spyod pa 'du shes dang /sems dang yid
99a8(phal chen): thams cad kyis rnam par dben pa'i gnas la spyod pa/lam thams cad blang pa med pa dang dor ba med pa'i gnas la rnam para spyod pa/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pazab mo la 'jug pa'i gnas la rnam paraspyod
99b1(phal chen): bsgo kun nas chos kyi dbyings rgyas par 'gengs pa'i thabs kyi gnas la rnam par spyod pa/nyon mongs pa thams cad zhi zhing nye bar zhi ba'i gnas la rnam par spyod pa/lta pa dang sred pa dang nga rgyal thams cad spangs
99b2(phal chen): pa'i shes rab shes che ba'i gnas la rnam par sbyod pa/bsam gtan dang/rnam par thar pa dang/ting nge 'dzin dang/snyoms par 'jug pa dang/mngon par shes pa dang/rig pa bskyed pa thams cad
99b3(phal chen): la rnam par rol pa'i gnas la rnam par spyod pa/byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyi ting nge 'dzin gyi spyod yul bsgom pa'i gnas la rnam par spyod pa rnams kyi gnas so//'di ni sangs rgyas thams cad
99b4(phal chen): yongs su gnon pa'i shugs drag po'i snying po zhes bya ba'o//rigs kyi bu de dag ni byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi byang chub sems dpa'i skye ba btsa rnams te//de dag gis byang chub sems dpa' rnam sa skye bar
99b5(phal chen): 'gyur ba'o//'byung bar 'gyur ba'o//yang dag par 'phel bar byed ba'o//yang dag par 'phil bar byed pa'o//rab tu rdzogs par byed pa'o//yongs su grub par byed pa'o//zhing ma lus pa thams cad khong
99b6(phal chen): du chud par bya ba'i phyir rgyan gcig gi lhab lhub la rgyan dang/lhab lhub gyi rgyud rnam pa sna tshogs byin gyis rlob pa'o//sems can gyi khams gdul pa rgyun mi gcad pa'i phyir phyi ma'i mtha'i bskal pa'i mur 
99b7(phal chen): thug par byin gyi rlabs kyis rgyun 'chad pa med par byed pa'o//chos rgya mtsho thams cad gyi dmigs pa sna tshogs dang/shin tu bstan pa gcig nas gcig tu rgyud pa sna tshogs du ma rnam pa tha dad pa dang/
99b8(phal chen): chos mtha' yas pa gcig nas gcig tu rgyud pa'i rgyun mngon par 'tshang rgya bar byed pa'o//sangs rgyas kyi khyu mchog gi mthu phyung ba bsam gyis mi khyab pa chos kyi dbyings kyis klas pa/nam mkha'i dbyings
100a1(phal chen): @#//kyis mtha' gtugs pa yongs su ston pa'o//sems can tshad med pa spyod pa rgya mtsho sna tshogs dang ldan pa yongs su smin par bya ba dang /gdul bas yongs su 'dus pa rnams la chos kyi 'khor lo 
100a2(phal chen): skor ba yongs su ston pa'o//sem can gyi khams thams cad sangs rgyas 'byung ba dang /mi 'bral bar byin gyis rlob pa'o//chos thams cad kyi sprin dbyangs kyi yan lag rgya mtsho brjod du med pas rnam

100a8(phal chen): ma lus kun bkang ste//zhing dang sems can de bzhin chos dang sangs rgyas rnams//sems can dgrol phyir smon lam 'debs pas brgyan pa'i sems//de ni bsam gyis mi khyab rnams kyi skye ba gnyis//
100b1(phal chen): gang rnams chos kyi sprin 'thung nam yang mi ngoms shing //nges par rtogs pa'i yid kyis dus gsum chags med lus//mkha' dbyings dri med mnyam med rnams kyi lus ldan pa//mtshungs pa med pa de ni 
100b2(phal chen): de dag skye ba gsum//lhag pa'i bsam pa ri rab rdo rje mtshungs brtan pas//thams cad mkhyen tshul rgya mtshor rnam par 'jug byed cing //gang rnams snying rje chen po'i gang chen mtshor 'jug pa//de ni

100b4(phal chen): de ni skyes bu chen po de dag skye ba lnga//chos kyi rang bzhin rab tu rtogs pas chags med sems//dus gsum dpe' zla med pa'i sangs rgyas rigs byung zhing //gang rnams chos dbyings rgya mtsho'i tshul la 'jug byed pa//

100b6(phal chen): de ni mkhas pa rnams kyi skye ba bsam yas bdun//gang rnams ye shes rgya mtsho'i tshul la dbang ldan zhing /thams cad mkhyen pa'i sgo tshul rnam par dbyong byed la//ting 'dzin kun tshul gang chen mtsho la yongs 'jug pa//
100b7(phal chen): de ni de bzhin nyid rten rnams kyi skye ba brgyad//gang rnams zhing kun 'byam klas yongs su sbyong byed cing //sems can thams cad yongs su smin byed 'dul la rab brtson la//sangs rgyas rnam par 'phrul pa'i rgyan rnams rnam 
100b8(phal chen): ston pa//de ni grags pa chen po rnams kyi skye ba dgu//gang rnams rgyal ba'i stobs rnams ji bzhin rab zhugs te//thams cad mkhyen pa'i shugs drag yangs pa rnam spel cing /gang rnams chos dbyings gzhi 
101a1(phal chen): @#//tshul tha dad chags med pa//de ni rgyal sras rnams kyi skye ba bcu yin no//rigs kyi bu skye ba bcu po de dag gis byang chub sems dpa' rnams de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs su skye'o//de ltar na 'jig
101a2(phal chen): rten thams cad la snang ba byed pa yin no//rigs kyi bu kho mo ni byang chub sems dpa'i rnam par thar pa bskal pa tshad med par dmigs pa thams cad du byang chub sems dpa'i skye bas rnam par 'phral pa yongs su ston pa'o/
101a3(phal chen): smras pa 'phags ma byang chub sems dpa'i rnams par thar pa bskal pa tshadamed pa thams cad du byang chubasems dpa'i skye bas rnams par 'phrul pa yongs su ston pa 'di'i yul ji lta bu zhig mchis//smras pa rigs kyi bu
101a4(phal chen): khe mo ni byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyi skye ba yongs su lta zhing nye bar 'gro ba'i smon lam yongs su grub pa ste//rigs kyi bu de ltar kho mo ni bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyi skye ba rgya mtsho shin du yangs pa 
101a5(phal chen): la 'jug cing khong du chud pa ste/'di ltar stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams 'di'i byang chub sems dpa'i skyes khong du chud par byed cing /gling bzhi pa skal ba dang ldan pa'i 'dzam bu'i gling pa de'i lum bi
101a6(phal chen): ni'i nags tshal du byang chub sems dpa' 'byung ba yongs su blta ba'i don du sngon gyi smon lam gyis skyes te/de ltar kho mo 'di ni byang chub sems dpa'a'abyung ba'i rjes su dran pa bsgos zhing rnam par spyod do//
101a7(phal chen): de ltar kho mo 'di ni gnas te/de phan chang la brgya na bcom ldan 'das dga' ldan gyi pho brang nas 'pho bar 'gyur ro zhes bya ba'i tshe/lum bi ni'i nags tshal 'dir sngon gyi ltas bcu byung bar gyur te/bcu gang zhe na/
101a8(phal chen): 'di ltar lum bi ni'i nags tshal 'ad thams cad mthon dman med la/dam grog dang g.yangs med cing mnyam par gnas par gyur pa 'di ni sngon gyi ltas dang po byung ba'o//yang lum bi ni'i nags tshal la 'di thams cad
101b1(phal chen): gseg ma dang /gyo mo med la/steng du ma dang tsher ma dang bral ste/sa'i gzhi rdo rje las grub par gyur nas/rin po che mang pos yongs su gang bar gnas par gyur pa 'di ni sngon gyi ltas gnyis pa byung ba'o//
101b2(phal chen): yang lum bi ni'i nags tshal 'di thams cad rin po che thams cad kyi shing dang stsadang /ta la'i phreng bas rnam par 'khrigs pas yongs su brgyan par kun nas gnas par gyur pa 'di nisngon gyi ltas gsum pa byung ba'o//
101b3(phal chen): yang yum bi ni'i nags tshal 'dithams cad lha rdzas las kyang 'das pa'i spos kyi myu gu kun nas 'thon pa dang /bye ma thams cad kyi mdzod  kun nas 'thon pa dang/rgyal mtshan thams cad kyi sprin dang na bun yongs su
101b4(phal chen): byung ba dang/spos kyi rin po che'i gzugs kyis shing dung kun nas 'khor te/rgyan yongs su sbyangs par kun nas gnas par gyur pa 'di ni sngon gyi lhas bzhi pa byung ba'o//yang lum bi ni'i nags tsha la 'di thams
101b5(phal chen): cad lha rdzas gyi me tog dang /phreng ba dang /lhab lhub sna tshogs kyi mdzod rnam pa thams cad du yongs su rgyas pa dang ldan par kun nas gnas para'agyur pa 'di ni sngon gyi ltas lnga pa byung ba'o//yang lumabi ni'i 
101b6(phal chen): nags tshal 'di thams cad kyi shing thams cad la nor bu rin po che dam pa'i mdzod mngon par grub par gyur pa 'di ni sngon gyi lta sa drug pa byung ba'o//yang lum:bi ni'i nags tshal 'di thams cadakyi pad mo'i

112b3(phal chen): grong khyer chen po ser skya'i gnas gang na ba der phyin nas/byang chub sems dpa'i rnam par thar pa bskal pa tshad med par dmigs pa thams cad du byang chub sems dpa'i skye bas rnam par 'phrul pa yongs su ston

113a3(phal chen): cing rnam par dag pa'i spyod yul can khyod la bltas na/khyod ni ring po mi thogs par de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku dang /gsung dang /thugs kyi rgyan rnam par dag pa bla na med pa rab tu thob ste mtshan dang dpe 
113a4(phal chen): byad bzang pos rab tu brgyan pa'i lus dang stobs bcu'i ye shes snang bas brgyan pa'i sems kyis 'jig rten du rnam par spyod par 'gyur ro//khyod kyi brtson 'grus dang rnam par gnon pa las bltas na//

113a7(phal chen): de ltar khyod dge ba'i bshes gnyen gyi drung du 'gro ba dang /blta ba dang bsnyen bkur ba dang /gdams ngag ma nor bar blang ba dang /de'i yon tan la nan tan du bya ba'i tshul la brtson par byed pas yongs su mi 
113a8(phal chen): skyo//phyir myi ldog/sgyid lug par mi 'gyur te/khyod la bgegs sam/bar du gcod pa 'am/sgrib pa gang gis kyang mi tshugs te/bdud dam bdud kyi ris su gtogs pa'i lha rnams kyang mdun 
113b1(phal chen): na gnas pa med pas/de'i phyir khyod ni ring por mi thogs par sems can thams cad dga' bar byed bar 'gyur ro//de skad ces smras pa dang /tshong dpon gyi bu nor bzangs kyis byang chub sems dpa' yongs

113b3(phal chen): zhi bas dga' bar gyur te/sems can thams cad kyi las nyam de ba'i rnam par smin pa'i phyir ldog pa dang /sems can thams cad kyis bde ba 'grub pa dang /sems can tham sa cad kha na ma tho ba med pa'i 
113b4(phal chen): las la nan tan tu byed pas dga' ba dang dam pa nyams su tshor bar 'gyur ro//nam sems can de dag las dang nyon mongs pa rnam pa sna tshogs mi bde ba'i sbyor bas sems rnam par 'khrugs shing ngan song 
113b5(phal chen): du lhung bar gyur ba 'am/bde ba'i rgyud na gnas kyang lus dang sems kyi sdug bsngal dang yid mi bde ba sna tshogs nyams su nyod bar gyur cing /mi dga' bar gnas pa de'i tshe/byang chub sems dpa' rnams 
113b6(phal chen): kyang shing tu yid mi bde bar 'gyur ro//lha mo 'di lta ste dper na mi shin tu dang gis byams pa zhig la bu cig pa sdug cing yid du 'ong ba zhig bdog par gyur la/de'i yan lag dang nyid lag bcad pa zhig

114a1(phal chen): @#//bar len pa'i rgyus lus zhig nas bde ba'i rgyud mtho ris kyi 'jig rten du skye ba ma gyur tam/lha dang mi'i lam gyi rgyud du lus dang sems kyi bde ba nyams su myong ba zhig du gyur na/de'i tshe byang chub sems 
114a2(phal chen): dpa' shin tu bde ba dang ldan zhing /nyams bde ba dang yid dga' ba dang /rab tu spro ba dang /dga' ba'i yid nyams su bde ba dang ldan par 'gyur ro//lha mo byang chub sems dpa' rnams ni bdag gi don gyi 
114a3(phal chen): phyir thams cad mkhyen pa nyid du smon cing don du gnyer pa ma yin te/'khor ba'i dga' ba dang bde ba sna tshogs nyams su myong bar bya ba'i phyir ma yin/'dod pa'i khams su gtogs pa'i dga' ba rnam pa tha dad 
114a4(phal chen): pa sna tshogs don du gnyer bas ma yin/'du shes dang sems dang lta ba phyin ci log tu gyur ba'i dbang gis ma yin/kun tu sbyor ba dang /'ching ba dang /bag la nyal ba dang /kun nas 'khor ba'i dbang du

114b1(phal chen): rnam par bzlog pa'i don du thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes sgrib pa med pa yongs su tshol zhing de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad mchod pa dang /bsnyen pa'i smon lam mngon par bsgrub po//de dag de bzhin gshegs

114b7(phal chen): byang chub sems dpa'i spyod ba mtha' dang dbus med pa la spyod de/de ltar spyod pa ni lha dang mi'i phun sum tshogs pa yongs su skyed bas lha dang bcas pa'i 'jig rten gyi rgyan du gyur pa yin/byang chub tu sems 
114b8(phal chen): bskyed pa la rab tu 'god pa'i phyir pa dang mar gyur pa yin/byang chub sems dpa'i lam du 'jud pas ma ma lta bur gyur pa yin/ngan song gi g.yang sa'i 'jigs pa las srung ba'i phyir ldan cig skyes 
115a1(phal chen): @#//pa'i lha rtag tu phyi bzhin 'phrang ba lta bur gyur pa yin/'khor ba'i rgya mtsho las sgrol ba'i phyir mnyan pa chen po lta bur gyur pa yin//bdud dang nyon mongs pa'i 'jig rten thams cad rnam par bzlog pa'i

115a3(phal chen): skyel bas mgon myed par gyur pa yin/sangs rgyas kyi yon tan thams cad kyi me tog gis sems yongs su rgyas par byed pas me tog tu gyur pa yin/bsod nams dang ye shes kyi 'od yangs pa rab tu 'gyed pas 
115a4(phal chen): rgyan du gyur pa yin/kun nas mdzes pas dga' ba dang bde ba dam pa byed pa yin/kha na ma tho ba med pa'i las la nan tan du byed pas phrad na legs pa yin/yan lag dam pa rnam pa thams cad du yongs su chad 
115a5(phal chen): pa'i lus dang ldan pas kun tu bzang ba yin/blta na mi 'thun pa med pas gtad mi bra ba'i gzugs dang ldan pa yin//ye shes kyi 'od gzer rab tu 'gyed pa'i phyir 'od byed pa yin/chos kyi sgron ma 'dzin pa'i

115b1(phal chen): stong phrag bcu dang bcas pas/tshong dpon gyi bu nor bzangs la lha rdzas las kyang 'das pa'i me tog dang phreng ba dang /bdug spos dang /phye ma dang byug pa dang /rin po che'i rgyan gyi char yid kyi rang bzhin mngon par 
115b2(phal chen): phab ste/kun nas bskor cing rjes su 'brangs nas/byang chub sems dpa'i khang par 'gro ba'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag gis mngon par bstod do//byang chub rdzogs par thugs bskyed cing //

115b8(phal chen): ser bu ji bzhin no//bskal pa'i me 'bar me bzhin du//rtag tu 'bar bas rab tu brtson//me lta bu'i brtson 'grus kyis//byang chub spyad pa spyod par mdzad//seng ge bzhin du shin tu dpa'//

117b3(phal chen): bsam pa rlabs po che rnam par dag pa dang /bsod nams dang ye shes rgya mtsho yangs pas rab tu bstan pa'i sems dang /sangs rgyas skye ba 'byung ba'i chos bstan pa chen po thos pa rab tu thob pa dang /dus gsum gyi

191a8(phal chen): dang //rgyal ba'i spyod yul thams cad yongs su 'jal mdzad cing //bskal pa bye bar rnam par spyod byed kyang //yongs su doms par 'gyur ba'i dus med do//'dir ni ting 'dzin bya ba'i rnams//brjod du med pa'i skad cig re rer rtogs byas shing //
191b1(phal chen): ting nge 'dzin gyi tshul bzhin du//sangs rgyas yul rnams rnam par ston pa mdzad//'dir ni skad cig brjod med pa'i//bskal pa zhing rnams sangs rgyas mtshan la rab bzhugs te//shin tu yangs pa'i blo ldan pa//bskal pa dpag tu med 
191b2(phal chen): pa'i mthar yang 'byin//'di ni gnas shing tshad med pa'i//bskal par sems rgyud gcig gis rab tu 'jug par byed//yongs rtogs 'du shes med bral yang //'gro ba'i 'du shes dpal du ston pa byed//de dag ting 'dzin 'di nas 
191b3(phal chen): su//zhugs kyang dus gsum thams cad gsal bar mngon tu mthong //skad cig yud la gnas brten cing //rnam thar gnas la rnam par spyod pa mdzad//de dag rnam spyod gnas 'di na//ma g.yos lus kyis skyil mo krung du 'khod 
191b4(phal chen): bzhin du//zhing rnams thams cad kun tu yang //thams cad khyab par cig char yongs su snang //khyu mchog de dag 'di na gnas//bde gshegs rnams las chos kyi rgya mtsho yang dag 'thung //ye shes rgya mtshor rab tu zhugs//
191b5(phal chen): mi zad yon tan rnams kyi pha rol phyin//'di ni zhing rnams kun grangs dang //bskal pa'i grangs dang de bzhin chos kyi grangs rnams dang //rgyal ba'i kun gyi mtshan gyi grangs//sgrib med sems pa rnam par dgongs pa 
191b6(phal chen): mdzad//rgyal sras de dag 'di na gnas/dus gsum zhing mnga' grangs pa thams cad ma lus par//de dag skad cig gcig gis kyang //yongs su 'byung dang 'jig pa rnam par 'byed//rgyal sras de dag 'dir na gnas//
191b7(phal chen): rgyal ba'i spyod pa smon lam 'gro ba'i dbang po rnams//gnas ni rnam par spyod byed kyang //rgyal sras blo la thogs pa med pas mthong //rdul phran gcig la gang yod pa'i//'khor rnams rgya mtsho zhing mang sems can de bzhin 
191b8(phal chen): du//bskal pa rdul phran kun mnyam pa//bsgribs pa med par mthong ba rnams kyis mthong //rdul phran kun rtser de bzhin du//'khor dang zhing dang bskal pa thams cad ma lus pa//kun kyang gzugs brnyan thabs tshul du 
192a1(phal chen): @#//shin tu rnam phye yongs su mthong ba yin//'di nas de dag chos rnams kyi//rang bzhin zhing rnams kun dang dus dang bskal pa dang //yang dag sangs rgyas de bzhin te//rang bzhin dngos bral 'byung tshul gyis mkhyen pa 
192a2(phal chen): 'di gnas sems can mnyam nyid dang //chom rnams mnyam pa nyid dang sangs rgyas mnyam nyid dang //zhing dang smon lam mnyam pa nyid//dus gsum mnyam pa nyid la'ang de bzhin 'jug/sems can khrag khrig rnam par 
192a3(phal chen): 'dul//sangs rgyas khrag khrig rnams kyang de bzhin gzhan mchod byed//gzhan rnams chos la rnam par rtog/'on kyang brtan pa gnas mchog 'di na 'khod//bskal pa khrag khrig mang pos kyang //de dag smon lam ye shes 
192a4(phal chen): blo yi rnam pa dag/bdag gis brjod par mi nus te//mtha' yas blo mnga' rnams kyi shin tu yangs//de dag smon pa mi mnga' ba//bsgribs med spyod yul rab la shin tu dgyes pa yi//pho brang 'di la thal sbyar te//
192a5(phal chen): bdag kyang lus btud gus par phyag 'tshal lo//rgyal ba'i sras kyi thu bo ste//bsgribs med spyod pa mnga' ba 'phags pa byams pa de//rnam dag blo mnga' dpe med la//de'i rjes su bsams te phyag 'tshal lo//
192a6(phal chen): de nas tshong dpon gyi bu nor bzangs kyis khang pa brtsegs pa chen po rnam par snang mdzad kyis brgyan pa'i snying po na 'khod pa'i byang chub sems dpa' rnams la//de dag dang byang chub sems dpa'i bstod pa gzhan tshad med pa dag 
192a7(phal chen): gis kyang //mngon par bstod de shin tu btud nas phyag 'tshal te/rab tu gus par bltas nas rim gror byas te/mngon tu byas nas mchod de khang pa brtsegs pa chen po'i rnam par snang mdzad kyi rgyan gyis brgyan pa'i snying po'i sgo na 
192a8(phal chen): byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po byams pa la blta bar ni mos//byang chub sems dpa' semasadapa' chen po byams pa ding ni phrad bar dga' zhing sdod pa las des byang chub sems dpa' byams pa khang pa brtsegs pa 
192b1(phal chen): de'i phyi rol gyi phyogs gzhan zhig nas srog chags brgya stong du ma'i 'khor dang lha dang klu dang /gnod sbyin dang /dri za dang /lha ma yin dang /nam mkha' lding dang /mi'am ci dang /lto 'phye chen po'i dbang po du mas mdun bu byas te/
192b2(phal chen): g.yas phyogs nas brgya byin dang /tshangs pa dang 'jig rten gyi mgon po rnams kyis phyag bya zhing yul ne na gnas pa gnyen gshes su 'ong ba'i bram ze brgya stong mang pos bskor cing mdun du byas te/khang pa brtsegs pa rnam par snang 
192b3(phal chen): mdzad kyi rgyan gyis brgyan pa'i snying po logs su 'ong ba mthong nas dga' rang sa te/mgu zhing nyams pa de la tshim zhing yid bde ba skyes nas/byang chub sems dpa' byams pa ga na ba de logs su mngon du bltas te/thag ring 
192b4(phal chen): po nas byang chub sems dpa' byamasapa la lus thams cad kyis phab ste phyag byas so//de nas byang chub sems dpa'i byams pas tshong dpon gyi bu nor bzangs la bltas te/'khor kun la lag pa g.yas pas bstan nas/
192b5(phal chen): yang dag pa'i yon tan gyis bsngags te tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//ltos shig bsam pa yongs su dag pa 'di//nor bzangs nor rnams rab tu brtan pa'i du//byang chub spyod par ma pa yongs tshol zhing /
192b6(phal chen): mkhas pa nga'i drung du nye bar 'ongs//legs par 'ongs sam snying rje byams byung khyod//byams pa'i dkyil 'khor yangs pa legs 'ongs sam//legs pas 'ongs sam blta na rab zhib//spyad pa spyod tshe dub par ma 
192b7(phal chen): gyur tam//rnam dag bsam ldan tshur shog 'ongs pa legs//skyon med yid ldan tshur shog 'ongs pa lags//dbang po ma zhum tshur shog 'ongs pa legs //mkhas pa sbyor tshe dub pa med grang ngo//chos rnams 
192b8(phal chen): thams cad rnam phye'i don byung ba//'gro ba thams cad rnam gdul rab brtson pa//bshes gnyen thams cad bsnyen bkur rab zhugs pa//brtul zhugs mi g.yo brtan pa khyod 'ongs legs//dge ba'i lam gyis 
193a1(phal chen): @#//'ongs pa bder 'ongs sam//yon tan lam la gnas pa bder 'ongs sam/rgyal ba'i lam la zhugs pa bder 'ongs sam/lam rnams gang du'ang dal bar ma gyur tam//yon tan rang bzhin tshur 
193a2(phal chen): shog bder 'ongs sam//dge bas mngon brlan tshur shog bder 'ongs sam/mtha' yas spyod yul legs par bder 'ongs sam//'gro ba kun gyis khyod mthong shin tu dkon//thob dang ma thob mnyam pa'i yid ldan pa//
193a3(phal chen): smad dang sdug bsngal ma grags rnam par spangs//'jig rten chos la ud dpal pad mo bzhin//yid la 'khrul med legs par bder 'ongs sa ma//g.yo dang sgyu bral bsam pa dge ldan pa//nga rgyal dregs med snod kyi dam pa 
193a4(phal chen): po/khro dang tshig med khengs shing 'gying pa med//blta na sdug pa khyod mthong legs pa yin//tshur shog phyogs kun spyod yul mngon gyur pa//tshur shog rgyal ba'i mdzod rnams sgrub byed pa//tshur shog rgyal ba'imadzod rnams 
193a5(phal chen): 'phel byed pa//yid la dub med legs par khyod 'ongs sam//tshur shog dus gsum yul ldan 'ongs pa legs//chos kyi dbyings la mos pa'i dkyil 'khor ldan//sangs rgyas kun gyi yon tan snying por byung //mkhas pa 
193a6(phal chen): legs par 'ongs sam mi dal lam//tshur shog 'jam dpal ye shes pad mo'i mchog/tshur shog sprin dpal char bas rnam spel ba//rgyal ba kun gyi sras pos btang ba tshur//khyod la bsgribs pa'i med pa'i phyogs kyang bstan//ltos shig 
193a7(phal chen): smon lam dra ba ci 'dra ba//chos dbyings rgyas 'gengs bsam gyis mi khyab ste//byang chub sems dpa'i spyod la lam bsgrub pa//rgya che yangs byed nor bzangs 'dir 'ongs so//bde bar gshegs pa'i spyod 
193a8(phal chen): yul tshol byed cing //rdul dang bral ba'i spyod pa bgro ba'i phyir//smon lam brlabs chen yongs su 'dri byed cing //skyo ba'i yid med 'di ni 'dir 'ongs so//'das pa'i mgon pos gang la bslabs mdzad de//de bzhin ma byon 
193b1(phal chen): rnams kyang slob 'gyur la//bde gshegs bzhugs pa'i spyod par bya ba rnams//yongs su dri phyir 'di ni 'dir 'ongs so//'di ni bdag gi dge bshes chos smra pa//chos kun nan tan yongs su ston byed pa//byang chub sems 
193b2(phal chen): dpa'i spyod lam yongs ston pa//yin zhes sems gcig bsams nas 'dir 'ongs so//byang chub sems dpa' bdag gi blo skyed pa//sangs rgyas sras po byang chub bskyed byed pa//'di dag bdag bshes sangs rgyas bsngags pa zhes 
193b3(phal chen): de ltar dge ba'i sems kyis 'dir 'ongs so//'di dag bskyed pas bdag gi ma lta bu//yon tan 'o ma ldud pas ma ma 'dra//byang chub yan lag yongs su skyong ba yin//dge bshes 'di dag gnod pa rnam par zlog/rga shi thar byed 
193b4(phal chen): sman pa lta bu ste//bdud rtsi'i cher 'bebs brgya byin lha dbang 'dra//zla ba lta bu'i dkar po'i phyogs kyis rgyas//zhi ba'i phyogs stobs nyi ma gsal ba 'dra//dgra dang 'grogs la ri bo 'dra ba ste//rgya mtsho bzhin du mi 'khrugs sems dang 
193b5(phal chen): ldan//yongs su skyong dang mnyan pa 'dra ba zhes//de 'dra'i sems kyis nor bzangs 'dir 'ongs so//'jigs med skyob pas dpa' bo lta bu ste//ded dpon mgon dang skyabs su gyur pa yin//'di dag bdag la bde stobs dmigs 
193b6(phal chen): yin zhes//de 'dra'i sems kyi dge bshes bsnyen bkur byed//chos kyi phyogs rnams rtag tu ston pa ste//sangs rgyas yon tan ye shes ston pa yin//ngan song ngan 'gro thams cad sbyong bar byed//dge bshes 'di dag bdag la 
193b7(phal chen): dge ba ston//'di dag rgyal ba'i mdzod kun stobs pa yin//'di dag rgyal ba kun gyi mdzod srungs ste//'di dag rgyal ba kun kyi gsang 'dzin ces//de ltar mkhas pa 'di ni dge bshes snyen//ye shes phun ldan 'di las rnam par 
193b8(phal chen): bdag/gzugs dang longs spyod tshe rigs phun sum tshogs//'di la phun sum tshogs kun dkon pa yin//bsam pa de 'dras 'di ni 'dir 'ongs so//bsam pa rlabs chen 'di la kun kyang ltos//mkhas pa'i dge bshes rnams 
194a1(phal chen): @#//la 'di ston cing //mkhas pa 'di blo ci 'dra skyed bskyed pa ltos//khyod kyis rtag du de ltar bslab par bya//'di ni sngon gyi bsod nams dge ba'i rgyus//byang chub zhugs te 'jam dpal mthong gyur nas//de'i gdams ngag rjes su 
194a2(phal chen): 'thun spyod cing //skyo ba med par rgyu ba 'di la ltos//'dis ni dga' bde thams cad kun spangs te//lha rdzas lta bu'i khyim yang yongs su spangs//ba ma ma ma skyid pa rgya che kun//yongs su spangs shing bran ltar bshes gnyen sten//
194a3(phal chen): mkhas pa 'dis ni bsam pa rnams sbyangs te//bdag gi ma lus yongs su spang byas nas//sangs rgyas kun gyi pho brang 'gro bar 'gyur//de phyir 'bras bu de 'dra 'ang thob par gyur//'dis ni rga dang nad kyis mnar ba yi//
194a4(phal chen): srog chags sdug bsngal brgya yis gces pa rnams//skye shi mya ngan 'jigs pas gdungs mthong nas//de dag don du snying rje'i sems kyis spyod//'gro rgyud lnga'i 'khor lo'i dkyil 'khor du//sdug bsngal 'khrul 'khor bcing 
194a5(phal chen): pa'i 'gro mthong nas//sdug bsngal 'khrul 'khor'khor lo 'dzom pa yi//ye shes rdo rje sra ba 'di tshol lo//'dod chags zhe sdang rtswa sdong cher mi can//lta chags rtsa mang yongs su 'jings pa yi//sems can zhing ni yongs 
194a6(phal chen): su sbyang ba'i phyir//shes rab gshol bstan bzang po 'di tshol lo//gti mug ma rig thibs po'i bsam pa can//'gro ba shes rab mig stor dmigs med pa//de la phyogs bde ston pa'i mthu yod pa'i//'gro ba'i ded 
194a7(phal chen): dpon bzang por 'di 'gyur ro//bzod pa'i go bgos rnam thar bzhon pa zhon//ye shes ral gyis mya ngan dgra 'dzom pas//mi 'jigs stobs pa'i dpa' bor gyur pa 'di//'gro ba rnams kyi lam ston 'gyur ba yin//
194a8(phal chen): 'di ni chos kyi grur ba sbyor ba yin//ye shes rgya mtsho'i lam la bslabs pa'o//zhi ba rin chen mchog gi gling skye ba//'di ni khams gsum mtsho'i skye ba 'dzin//ye shes 'od gzar smon lam dkyil 'khor can//sems can 
194b1(phal chen): kun gyi gnas su snang byed pa'i//chos dbyings mkha' la 'od chen rab ldan par//'di ni sangs rgyas nyi mar 'char bar 'gyur//byams pa'i tsan dan ting 'dzin bsil ba yi//sems can kun la snyoms sems 'od bzang 
194b2(phal chen): ba/dkar po'i chos kyis dkyil 'khor yongs rgyas pa'i//sangs rgyas zla bar 'di ni 'char bar 'gyur//bsam pa'i bzhi rten brtan la rab gnas pa//byang chub spyod pas rim gros 'phags pa ste//chos kun rin chen

194b4(phal chen): 'di ni 'gro bar chos sprin char 'bebs 'gyur//dag pa'i snying po'i dri gsum mun sel ba//byams pa'i snum yod dran pa'i snod brtan la//dri med byang chub sems med 'od bzang ba'i//chos kyi mar me 'di ni 'bar byed 
194b5(phal chen): 'gyur//byang chub sems ni mar me snying rje skyo//byams pa rdos pa mi g.yo bsam pa la brags//byang chub yan lag mthar gyis 'byung ba yi//sangs rgyas sras su 'di ni yang dag skye//bsod nams snying po mngon 
194b6(phal chen): par 'phel bar byed//ye shes snying po yongs su spyod par 'gyur//'di ni shes pa'i snying po 'char bar byed//smon lam ci bzhin snying po yang dag byung //snying rje byams pas sprul pa 'di 'dra ba//sems can dgrol blo 
194b7(phal chen): phan pa'i bsam ldan pa//rnam dag yid can 'di ni ci 'dra ba//de 'dra'i 'gro ba lha dang mir bcas dkon//'di 'dra'i bsam pa rab tu rtsa brtan la//'di 'dra'i sbyor ba brtan pas rnam 'phel cing //srid gsum 
194b8(phal chen): g.yog par byed pa 'di 'dra ba'i//ye shes shing chen 'bras bu 'byung ba dkon//'di ni yon tan kun grub don du gnyer//chos rnams thams cad yongs su dri bor mos//the tsom thams cad rnam par bsal don 
195a1(phal chen): @#//du/le lo med par bshes gnyen thams cad ste//'di ni bdud kyi dkrugs ma nyon mongs 'jom//'di ni lta ba'i dri ma med par sel//'di ni 'gro ba thams cad dgrol la brtson//mkhas pa 'di

195b1(phal chen): sems can rgya mtsho rnam par 'grel par 'gyur//spyod pa rgya mtsho yongs su rdzogs par 'gyur//khyod kyi yon tan rgya mtsho de 'dra'o//khyod kyi mos pa'i dkyil 'khor tshul las ni//khyod ni yon tan rnams kyi snod du 'gyur//khyod ni dge ba

196a1(phal chen): @#//byed pa'i 'od ldan pa//sems can khams rnams bde byed 'od ldan pa//ngan song thams cad zhi byed 'od ldan pa'o//srid gsum sdug bsngal zhi bar byed par 'gyur//mtho ris lam ni nye bar ston par 'gyur//

196a3(phal chen): 'gyur//brtan pa'i blo gros le lo med pa khyod//mi g.yang byang chub lam ni de ltar tshol//srid pa'i rgya mtsho gnas pa'i lus can rnams//sdug bsngal mthar phyin bya bar khyod brtson pa//'gro ba srid pa'i mtsho las sgrol

196a6(phal chen): rgya mtsho'i mthus//zhing rnams rgya mtsho mang bar phyin par 'gyur//'khor rnams rgya mtsho mang ba mthong bar 'gyur//chos mang rgya mtsho yongs su 'thun par 'gyur//sangs rgyas sprin mang khrag khrig mthong bar 'gyur//mchod

216a1(phal chen): @#//gsum pa thams cad las rnam par 'das te/de bzhin gshegs pa'i ye shes nam mkha'i spyod yul 'dus ma byas pa thams cad kyi mthar thug par 'gyur ro//rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste dper na/rdo rje ni

252b4(phal chen): 'jig rten gsum po kun na 'ang mchod par 'gyur//byang chub shing dbang drung du de myur 'gro//song nas sems can phan phyir der 'dug ste//byang chub sangs rgyas 'khor lo rab tu bskor//bdud rnams sde dang bcas pa 
252b5(phal chen): thams cad btul//gang yang bzang po spyod pa'i smon lam 'di//'chang ba dang ni klog gam bston pa yi//de'i rnam par smyin pas sangs rgyas mkhyen//byang chub mchog la som nyi dam byed cig/'jam dpal ji 
252b6(phal chen): ltar mkhyen cing dpa' bo dang //kun tu bzang po de yang de bzhin te//de dag gi ni rjes su bdag slob cing //dge ba 'di dag thams cad rab tu bsngo//dus gsum gshegs pa rgyal ba thams cad kyis//bsngo 
252b7(phal chen): ba gang la mchog tu sngags pa des//bdag gi dge ba'i rtsa ba 'di kun kyang //bzang po spyod phyir rab tu bsngo bar bgyi//bdag ni 'chi ba'i dus byed gyur pa na//sgrib pa thams cad dag ni phyir bstsal te//mngon sum 
252b8(phal chen): snang ba mtha' yas de mthong nas//bde ba can gyi zhing der rab tu 'gro//der song nas ni smon lam 'di dag kyang //thams cad ma lus mngon du gyur par shog/de dag ma lus bdag gis yongs su bskang //'jig rten 
253a1(phal chen): ji srid sems can phan par bgyi//rgyal ba'i dkyil 'khor bzang zhing dga' ba der//pad mo dam pa shin tu mdzes las skyes//snang ba mtha' yas rgyal ba'i mngon 
253a2(phal chen): sum du//lung bstan pa yang bdag gis der thob shog/der ni bdag gis lung bstan rab thob nas//sprul pa mang po bye ba phrag brgya yis//blo'i stobs kyis phyogs bcu rnams su yang //sems can 
253a3(phal chen): rnams la phan pa mang po bgyi//gang gis bzang po spyod pa ston pa yis//dge ba chung zad bdag gis ci bsags pa//des ni 'gro ba'i smon lam dge ba rnams//skad cig gcig gis thams cad 'byor 
253a4(phal chen): par shog/gang yang bzang po spyod pa bsngos pa yis//bsod nams mtha' yas dam pa thob pa des//'gro ba'i sdug bsngal chu bor bying ba rnams//'od dpag med pa'i gnas rab 'gro bar shog/
253a5(phal chen): shin tu rgyas pa chen po'i mdo//sangs rgyas phal po che zhes bya ba las//byang chub sems dpa'i sde snod kyi nang nas//sdong pos brgyan pa zhes bya ba chos kyi rnam grangs chen po las/nor 
253a6(phal chen): bzangs kyis dge ba'i bshes gnyen bsnyen bkur ba'i spyod pa phyogs gcig te ci snyed pa rdzogs s+ho////
